TL;DR: 本文提出KITE框架，将工程先验知识（"语义先验"）通过可切换的协变量机制与注意力偏置机制注入自监督事件预测器，在稀缺标签下提升罕见高成本事件预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列中罕见高成本事件的前兆极少被观测，纯数据驱动模型缺乏可学习信号；而运维工程师掌握的先验知识（语义先验）不在稀缺标签中，尚未被有效利用。

Method: 将语义先验通过两种可切换机制注入自监督事件预测器：协变量机制与注意力偏置机制，二者在零值时均为恒等映射，因此同一架构无需逐数据集搜索即可适配各类先验。

Result: 先验仅在提供观测窗口未含信息时有效。短上下文下：周期性时钟先验使池化h-AUROC相对无先验基线提升14%（p<0.001）；守恒残差先验在0.25标签比例下提升47%；有效先验可跨域迁移（+53%），可替代特征迁移；窗口已能恢复的先验无增益，注意力偏置机制无可测收益。

Conclusion: KITE提供了可切换、可归因的上下文条件化路径，可作为向跨域、预训练时间序列基础世界模型扩展的基底，使模型显式承载其支配结构。

Abstract: Forecasting rare, high-cost events in multivariate time-series systems is hard because their precursors are seldom observed, so a purely data-driven model has little to learn from. The engineers who run these systems, however, hold knowledge that precedes such events, information absent from the scarce labels. We call this a semantic prior and inject it into a self-supervised event forecaster through two switchable mechanisms, a covariate mechanism and an attention-bias mechanism, each the identity at its null, so one architecture serves every prior type with no per-dataset architecture search. A prior helps when it supplies information the observed window does not already contain: in a short context an informative prior beats no prior in-domain (pooled h-AUROC over the no-prior baseline: a periodicity clock +14%, p<0.001; a conservation residual +47% at a 0.25 label fraction), and a working prior transfers out-of-domain (+53%), substituting for feature transfer, while priors the window already recovers stay inert and the attention-bias mechanism shows no measurable gain. KITE is a switchable, attributable route to contextual conditioning, and a substrate for scaling toward cross-domain, pretrained time-series foundation world models that carry their governing structure explicitly.

</details>


### [35] [Neural expectation-maximization and meta-models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12741)
*Zhuolin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将Transformer解释为一种混合概率推断过程的近似：即在结构与参数由数据推断的部分可观测马尔可夫随机场上，将max-product环状置信传播（LBP）与EM相结合。作者证明自注意力是输入条件化、动态结构MRF上max-product LBP的可微近似，并建立逐层深度的EM对应关系，进而提出"元模型"设计范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer网络经验上表现强大，但缺乏一个统一、可解释的概率推断理论框架，用以说明自注意力、深度堆叠等机制为何有效。作者希望从概率推断视角给出Transformer的机理解释，并把多种既有机制纳入同一理论体系。

Method: 理论上，将Transformer建模为对"部分可观测MRF"进行推断的近似，其中MRF的结构与参数由数据推断。证明自注意力实现输入条件化、动态结构MRF上max-product LBP的可微近似，注意力权重编码学到的结构亲和度。同时在深度层面建立EM对应：每层前向传播更新隐表示对应E步，跨层的参数转移θ^(l)→θ^(l+1)作为隐式M步。基于此提出meta-models范式，用输入生成实例特定参数，统一自注意力、专家混合与门控单元。

Result: 给出了自注意力与max-product LBP的可微近似的理论对应，以及逐层E步/M步的EM解释；提出meta-models框架，将自注意力、MoE与门控单元统一在"输入生成实例参数"的机制之下。摘要称实证结果支持该观点，但未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: Transformer可被视为在数据推断出的动态MRF上进行max-product LBP与EM混合推断的习得近似；该视角为理解Transformer结构提供了统一概率解释，并衍生出meta-models设计范式。未来价值在于指导更具可解释性与统一性的架构设计。

Abstract: We interpret Transformer networks as learned approximations to a hybrid probabilistic-inference procedure that combines max-product Loopy Belief Propagation (LBP) with Expectation--Maximization (EM). In this view, a Transformer performs inference in partially observed Markov Random Fields (MRFs) whose structure and parameters are not given a priori but are inferred from data. We show that self-attention implements a differentiable approximation to max-product LBP on an input-conditioned, dynamically structured MRF, with attention weights encoding learned structural affinities. We further establish an EM correspondence at the level of depth: each layer updates latent representations in an E-step-like forward pass, while the transition $θ^{(l)} \to θ^{(l+1)}$ across layers acts as an implicit M-step. Building on this interpretation, we introduce \emph{meta-models}, a design paradigm in which inputs generate instance-specific parameters, unifying mechanisms such as self-attention, mixture-of-experts, and gated units within a single framework. Empirical results support the proposed perspective.

</details>


### [36] [Static Gradient Attribution Underperforms a Density-Matched Random Mask on Loss-Based Forgetting Within LoRA B-Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12744)
*Yu Sun,Junwei Zhou,Zuodong Xiang,Yike Zhang,Pengcheng Xu,Xinyu Guan,Ruoyun Ma,Hailu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出在零初始化LoRA下比较梯度掩码与随机掩码存在参数池混淆，控制参数池后发现在Qwen2.5-3B上随机均匀掩码的基于损失的向后迁移持续低于梯度归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效持续学习常用梯度归因选择更新的适配器参数子集，但需额外反向传播；同时现有"梯度掩码优于随机掩码"的比较可能因参数池不同而不公平，需澄清选择准则本身的真实效果。

Method: 识别零初始化LoRA（B=0）下∂L/∂A=0导致梯度掩码只从B中选取、而随机掩码从A和B中选取的参数池混淆；通过将两种方法限制在同一参数池来隔离选择准则，在Qwen2.5-3B上以七个随机种子进行比较。

Result: 在受控比较下得到负面结果：均匀随机掩码的基于损失的向后迁移一致低于梯度归因；效应集中于医学任务（占总体80%），代码与数学同向，法律无可靠差异；两个离散选项任务（医学、法律）上平均准确率-BWT差异与零无统计显著区别。

Conclusion: 该优势与所测任务序列下的稳定性—可塑性权衡一致：随机掩码略微降低单任务拟合能力。原比较中的"梯度掩码更优"结论部分源于参数池混淆而非选择准则本身。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient continual learning updates a restricted subset of adapter parameters to incorporate sequential tasks while limiting catastrophic forgetting. Gradient attribution is a common rule for selecting that subset, but it requires an additional backward pass over calibration data. We identify a parameter-pool confound in comparisons between initialization-time gradient masks and unrestricted random masks under standard zero-initialized LoRA ($B=0$): the gradient with respect to $A$ is identically zero ($\partial\mathcal{L}/\partial A=0$), so gradient masks select strictly from the $B$ matrix whereas unrestricted random masks draw from both $A$ and $B$. Such a comparison changes both the selection rule and the candidate parameter pool. Isolating the selection criterion by restricting both approaches to the same parameter pool, we find a negative result: a uniform random mask consistently achieves lower loss-based backward transfer than gradient attribution across seven seeds on Qwen2.5-3B. The effect is concentrated on the medical task, which contributes 80\% of the aggregate; code and math move in the same direction, and legal shows no reliable difference. This advantage is consistent with a stability--plasticity trade-off under the tested task sequence, in which random masking slightly reduces single-task fitting. On the two discrete-option tasks (Medical and Legal), the mean accuracy-BWT difference is not statistically distinguishable from zero.

</details>


### [37] [NV-Tesseract-AD: Curriculum-Enhanced Conditional Diffusion for Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12754)
*Aditi Gautam,Manikandan Ravikiran,Saira Qureshi,Sena Fatma Ekiz,Charan Jagwani,Mahmoud Elhadidy,Sanjeev Roka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NV-Tesseract-AD，一种课程学习增强的条件扩散框架，用于多变量时间序列异常检测；通过掩码率与扩散时间步的“双轴三阶段课程”提升重构判别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心假设：重构难度可沿两条互补轴显式结构化（掩码严重程度、扩散噪声水平），沿两轴由易到难同步训练可稳定优化并提升残差可判别性。

Method: Transformer扩散骨干网络 + 掩码比例与扩散时间步的三阶段双课程；保留异常样本贯穿训练，利用结构化损坏驱动自监督表示学习。评估含分布内训练（TSB-UAD-M语料）与严格零样本迁移（十个EasyTSAD基准族，无逐数据集调参）。

Result: 双轴课程产生掩码不变重构（首阶段互补掩码策略的损失差距近零）；F1提升约3.7倍、MSE下降56%（相对非课程基线）。零样本F1：NAB达0.984，NormA达0.948。异质族表现较弱，归因于无监督阈值校准而非重构质量。DPM-Solver++使采样成本降低3.5至5.7倍（数据集层面），但与阈值行为耦合。

Conclusion: 将扩散去噪难度与课程学习原则对齐，为复杂多变量时间序列的鲁棒异常检测提供了有原则且实用的方法；阈值校准是当前主要检测瓶颈，采样器与阈值需联合验证。

Abstract: We propose NV-Tesseract-AD, a curriculum learning-enhanced conditional diffusion framework for multivariate time-series anomaly detection. Our central hypothesis is that reconstruction difficulty can be explicitly structured along two complementary axes, masking severity and diffusion noise level, and that training from easier to harder tasks along both axes simultaneously stabilizes optimization and improves residual discriminability. We integrate a Transformer-based diffusion backbone with a three-stage dual curriculum over masking ratios and diffusion timesteps, retaining anomalous samples throughout training and relying on structured corruption to drive self-supervised representation learning. We evaluate NV-Tesseract-AD under two regimes: in-distribution training on the TSB-UAD-M corpus and strict zero-shot transfer to ten EasyTSAD benchmark families without per-dataset tuning. The dual-axis curriculum produces masking-invariant reconstruction, confirmed by a near-zero loss gap between complementary masking strategies within the first training stage, and yields an approximately 3.7-fold improvement in F1 and a 56 percent reduction in MSE relative to a non-curriculum baseline. Zero-shot performance reaches F1 = 0.984 on NAB and F1 = 0.948 on NormA. Weaker results on heterogeneous families are traced to unsupervised threshold calibration rather than reconstruction quality, identifying calibration as the dominant remaining detection bottleneck. Accelerated inference via DPM-Solver++ reduces sampling cost by a factor of 3.5 to 5.7 at the dataset level but couples to thresholding behavior, establishing that the sampler and threshold must be validated jointly. Together, these findings demonstrate that aligning diffusion denoising difficulty with curriculum learning principles provides a principled and practical approach for robust anomaly detection in complex multivariate time series.

</details>


### [38] [Provenance-grouped protocol for evaluating machine learning in perovskite photovoltaics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12766)
*Qiwei Han,Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出钙钛矿太阳能电池机器学习模型常用的随机划分存在严重数据泄漏，提出分层分组划分协议并重新基准测试13个模型，发现模型排名在不同划分下发生关键转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿太阳能电池的机器学习模型通常采用随机数据划分进行评估，但近三分之二的器件与其他器件共享完全相同配方，导致随机划分主要奖励对历史数据的记忆而非真正的物理泛化能力，因此需要更严格的评估方法来区分记忆与真实发现。

Method: 作者梳理了Perovskite Database的机构来源，建立了一套严格的分组划分层级，逐步隔离重复器件、重复出版物和重复机构；随后在各类划分下对13个模型进行基准测试，并开展特征重要性分析。

Result: 在所有划分上13个模型均刷新了最优性能；模型排名出现关键转变——树集成模型在随机划分中因识别熟悉配方而占优，但在真正未见过的机构和未来发现上其排名崩塌，而成分感知的神经网络架构表现优异。特征重要性显示，器件结构和制备方法对最终效率的决定作用与吸收层化学性质同样强。

Conclusion: 该标准化协议有助于社区区分记忆与真实物理发现，为钙钛矿太阳能电池机器学习评估提供更严谨的基准。

Abstract: Machine-learning models for perovskite solar cells are typically evaluated using random data splits, which we show systematically leak information through massive internal duplication. Because nearly two-thirds of curated devices share their exact recipe with another, random splits primarily reward the mere recall of historical data rather than testing true physical generalisation. To solve this, we mapped the institutional provenance of the Perovskite Database and established a rigorous hierarchy of grouped splits that progressively isolate duplicate devices, publications, and institutions. Benchmarking thirteen models establishes new state-of-the-art performance across all splits and reveals a critical regime shift: while tree ensembles dominate random splits by recognising familiar recipes, their ranking collapses on genuinely unseen institutions and future discoveries, where composition-aware neural architectures excel. Furthermore, feature importance demonstrates that the device architecture and fabrication methodology dictate final efficiency just as strongly as the absorber chemistry. This standardised protocol empowers the community to separate memorisation from true physical discovery.

</details>


### [39] [REDaCT: Collapse-Free Unlearning in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12769)
*Joshua Maccoby,Wendy Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REDaCT，一种面向大型推理模型（LRM）的推理时遗忘框架，通过泄漏检测器与纠正嵌入优化，在移除遗忘集信息的同时避免推理崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LRM遗忘方法在移除遗忘集信息时会付出"推理崩溃"的代价：模型无法生成连贯的思维链（CoT），且不能正常终止推理过程。遗忘需同时覆盖推理轨迹与最终答案两个方面。

Method: REDaCT包含两个关键组件：(1) 二分类泄漏检测器，用于识别推理轨迹中泄漏遗忘集信息的句子；(2) 嵌入优化模块，离线学习一个纠正嵌入，在推理时于首个检测到的泄漏处注入，引导后续推理转向不泄漏遗忘集信息的句子。

Result: 实验表明，REDaCT完全避免了推理崩溃，相比最先进的遗忘方法显著提升了推理保持能力，同时保持完美的答案遗忘与近乎完美的CoT遗忘。

Conclusion: REDaCT通过检测-纠正的推理时干预范式，在LRM遗忘任务中实现了推理能力保持与遗忘效果的良好平衡。

Abstract: Unlearning in large reasoning models (LRMs) requires removing forget-set information from both the reasoning trace and the final answer. Existing approaches achieve this objective at the cost of reasoning collapse, where the model fails to produce a coherent chain-of-thought (CoT) and does not properly terminate its reasoning process. To address this challenge, we propose REDaCT (REgenerate via Detection and Corrective Tuning), an inference-time unlearning framework for LRMs. REDaCT consists of two key components: (1) a binary leak detector that identifies sentences in the reasoning trace that leak forget-set information, and (2) an embedding optimization module that learns a corrective embedding offline, such that when injected at the first detected leak during inference, it guides subsequent reasoning towards sentences that do not leak forget-set information. Experimental results show that REDaCT completely avoids reasoning collapse, substantially improving reasoning preservation compared to state-of-the-art unlearning methods while maintaining perfect answer forgetting and near-perfect CoT forgetting. Our code is available at: https://github.com/jmaccoby/redact.

</details>


### [40] [Coordinate-Free Automatic Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12776)
*Alex Guerra,Joshua Aduol,Yaqian Tang,Daniel Williams,Ryan P. Adams,Peter Orbanz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种与基无关的通用导数算子，可用现有AD原语表达，统一表示多种几何微分运算，并兼容JAX的批处理、编译与硬件加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AD框架隐式固定欧氏空间基来定义梯度与雅可比，天然只处理固定坐标数组，难以表达旋转、体积形式、曲率等几何对象；现有专用变通方法缺乏可复用性。

Method: 证明存在单一、与基无关的导数算子，可表示多种几何微分运算，并完全用现有AD原语实现；将其作为可复用变换，与JAX批处理、编译和硬件加速组合，几何以代数数据形式引入。

Result: 实验验证了该算子在多种流形算子间的复用、在时空逆问题流水线中的组合使用，以及在不同转子子群上的优化应用。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种通用、可复用的几何微分算子实现路径，使AD框架能自然处理几何对象，并保持高性能与可组合性。

Abstract: Automatic differentiation (AD) is a cornerstone of modern machine learning. Since all AD frameworks implicitly fix a basis of Euclidean space to define gradients and Jacobians, they natively process mathematical objects that are arrays of a fixed set of coordinates. This makes it awkward to express objects such as rotations, volume forms, and curvatures that arise, for example, in applications of AI to science. Bespoke workarounds lack reusability. We show that (i) a single, basis-independent derivative operator can represent many geometric differential operations, and (ii) this operator can be expressed entirely in terms of existing AD primitives. This reusable transformation can be composed with JAX batching, compilation, and hardware acceleration. Geometry enters as algebraic data. We experimentally demonstrate its reuse across different manifold operators, its composition through a spacetime inverse-problem pipeline, and its use in optimization over different rotor subgroups.

</details>


### [41] [The Expressive Power of Random-Feature MPNNs Across Graph Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12809)
*Robert R. Nerem,Mitchell Black,Antonis Vasileiou,Christopher Morris,Yusu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个"群体级"(population-level)框架研究连续随机节点特征对MPNN表达力的提升，证明此类MPNN无法实现尺寸一致( size-uniform)的通用逼近，并刻画了随机颜色与高阶WL方法之间的不可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续随机节点特征已被证实能提升MPNN表达力，但现有理论分析不够精细，无法刻画其局限——特别是跨图尺寸的函数逼近能力，以及在固定随机比特数下的逼近能力。

Method: 作者针对初始特征具有与图尺寸无关的Lipschitz界的MPNN(涵盖mean、max及order-normalized sum架构)，建立尺寸一致逼近的群体级框架。核心是证明连续随机特征可在期望输出上以任意小的均匀误差量化为有限多种颜色，从而将有限颜色可区分性的局限迁移到连续随机特征的尺寸一致逼近局限上。

Result: 基于该框架证明这些MPNN不具备尺寸一致通用性；量化分析了颜色数量与独立试验次数带来的表达力增益；确立了随机颜色MPNN与高阶Weisfeiler-Leman方法之间的不可比性；并给出基于color coding的树子图同构以及一个局部MST任务的有限颜色MPNN构造。

Conclusion: 连续随机节点特征并不能使MPNN获得尺寸一致通用逼近能力；其表达力提升有明确上界，且与高阶WL方法互不包含，为理解随机特征MPNN的理论边界提供了更精细的工具。

Abstract: Continuous random node features increase the expressivity of message-passing neural networks (MPNNs), but existing theoretical analyses are not fine-grained enough to capture their limitations, particularly in approximating functions across graph sizes or under a fixed number of random bits. We develop a population-level framework for size-uniform approximation. For MPNNs with a graph-size-independent Lipschitz bound in their initial features, including standard mean, max, and order-normalized sum architectures, we show that continuous random features can be quantized to finitely many colors with arbitrarily small uniform error in expected output. This reduction lets us transfer limitations on finite-color distinguishability to limitations on size-uniform approximation with continuous random features. We use this framework to prove that these MPNNs are not size-uniformly universal, analyze the expressivity gains from colors and independent trials, and establish incomparability between random-color MPNNs and higher-order Weisfeiler-Leman methods. We also give finite-color MPNN constructions for tree subgraph isomorphism via color coding and a local MST task.

</details>


### [42] [Deploying RDMA for Large-Scale Inference and Training Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12815)
*Yibo Huang,Yi Cui,Yukai Huang,Huaping Zhou,Jiancong Tong,Zhaoyu Wu,Gen Miao,Haoran Wei,Boyan Ding,Zhuo Jiang,Jianxi Ye,Yibo Zhu,Chuanxiong Guo,Yang Xu,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了在字节跳动参数服务器推理与训练系统中部署基于硬件RDMA的软件通信层ByteExpress的实践经验，总结出三条部署教训，并通过编译器感知分配器、零拷贝传输、QP复用、接收信用流控、软件发送窗口和RPC辅助恢复等机制解决实际生产难题，在生产环境取得显著延迟与CPU开销收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDMA虽能降低分布式机器学习系统的通信开销，但在生产中落地并非简单替换TCP即可。变长消息、并发连接、突发incast以及服务故障等问题，仅靠硬件卸载无法解决，因此需要面向真实生产负载研究软硬件协同的通信层设计。

Method: 提出并部署ByteExpress，一个运行在硬件RDMA之上的软件通信层，应用于字节跳动的参数服务器推理与训练系统。其核心机制包括：RDMA感知的内存分配器、零拷贝传输、QP多路复用、接收信用流控、软件发送窗口以及RPC辅助的故障恢复。同时总结三条部署经验：高效RDMA需跨应用层与传输层进行缓冲区管理；可扩展通信需协调管理连接、接收容量与在途数据；服务可用性需在传输层之上实现恢复。

Result: 在生产推理场景中，ByteExpress相比内核TCP将模型参数拉取延迟的均值与P99分别降低35.7%和43.7%，总CPU使用率降低10.69%；在训练场景中，相比TCP将模型参数推送延迟P99最多降低74.0%，且运行时未观察到PFC暂停。

Conclusion: 在真实生产系统中，RDMA的收益依赖于软硬件跨层协同设计：缓冲区管理、连接与流量协调以及传输层之上的恢复机制缺一不可。ByteExpress的部署验证了该思路在推理与训练负载下均能带来显著的延迟与CPU开销优化，同时避免PFC暂停，具备生产可行性。

Abstract: RDMA can reduce communication overhead in distributed machine learning systems, but realizing these benefits in production requires more than replacing TCP. Variable-size messages, concurrent connections, bursty incasts, and service failures introduce challenges that hardware offload alone cannot address. This paper reports our experience deploying ByteExpress, a software communication layer over hardware RDMA, in ByteDance's parameter-server-based inference and training systems. We present three lessons from the deployment: efficient RDMA requires buffer management across application and transport layers; scalable communication requires coordinated management of connections, receive capacity, and outstanding data; and service availability requires recovery above the transport layer. ByteExpress combines an RDMA-aware allocator, zero-copy transfers, QP multiplexing, receive-credit flow control, a software send window, and RPC-assisted recovery. In production inference, ByteExpress reduces mean and P99 model parameter fetch latency by 35.7% and 43.7%, respectively, and total CPU usage by 10.69% compared with kernel TCP. In training, ByteExpress reduces P99 model parameter push latency by up to 74.0% compared with TCP, with no PFC pauses observed at runtime.

</details>


### [43] [Inverse Synthesis of Shock Time Series from Shock Response Spectra Using Ensembles of Conditional Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12820)
*Adam Watts,Charles Young,Ryan Bowering,Andrew Jeon,Destry Newton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于循环解码器的改进CVAE框架，通过预测多尺度小波系数并经逆离散小波变换重建波形，实现由给定冲击响应谱（SRS）反演候选加速度波形；通过组合不同母小波与分解层级的独立模型构成集成，显著降低谱误差并大幅提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SRS仅刻画单自由度系统对瞬态加速度的峰值响应，波形到SRS的映射为多对一，因此反演本质上是病态问题，需要生成与给定谱一致的候选波形。既有直接时域CVAE在潜变量采样下多样性有限，且传统方法（如衰减正弦叠加SDS）推理代价高。

Method: 扩展早前CVAE框架，引入循环解码器输出多尺度小波系数，并经逆离散小波变换重建时域波形；针对单模型潜采样变异性不足，构建由不同母小波和不同分解层级独立训练模型组成的集成，每个成员对同一目标SRS生成各自候选波形。另开展条件设置研究，考察频率网格与编码方式的影响。

Result: 在四个留出基准数据集（两个实际冲击数据集、一个地震数据集、一个合成冲击数据集）上，所提小波模型相较于直接时域CVAE与SDS基线具有更低的平均与中位谱误差，并在公开反演SRS方法对比中表现更优；在Brake报道的合成目标上具竞争力。批处理下单模型推理约0.1–0.6 ms/波形，SDS约需5–8 s（不含离线训练）。

Conclusion: 小波域循环CVAE集成可低成本、快速地生成多样且谱一致的候选波形，但谱吻合之外的物理适用性仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: The shock response spectrum (SRS) characterizes the peak responses of single-degree-of-freedom systems to transient acceleration. Because the mapping from an acceleration time series (waveform) to its SRS is many-to-one, inverse synthesis seeks candidate waveforms consistent with a prescribed spectrum. We extend an earlier conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) framework with a recurrent decoder that predicts multiscale wavelet coefficients and reconstructs waveforms through an inverse discrete wavelet transform. Individual models exhibit limited variability under latent sampling, motivating an ensemble of independently trained models spanning different mother wavelets and decomposition levels. Each member produces a separate candidate waveform for the same target SRS. Evaluation on four held-out benchmark datasets shows lower mean and median spectral errors for the illustrated wavelet model than for the earlier direct time-domain CVAE and a sum-of-decayed-sinusoids (SDS) baseline. A conditioning study examines frequency-grid and encoding choices. Comparisons with published inverse-SRS methods demonstrate superior performance across four holdout benchmark datasets (two operational shock datasets, one earthquake dataset, and one synthetic shock dataset) and competitive performance on synthetic targets reported by Brake. Single-model inference requires approximately 0.1-0.6 ms per waveform during batched inference compared with 5-8 s for SDS, excluding offline training. This low inference cost enables rapid generation of distinct SRS-compatible candidate waveforms across the ensemble, although their physical suitability requires assessment beyond spectral agreement.

</details>


### [44] [TreeHCA: Hindsight-Guided Exploration and Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12828)
*Debajoy Mukherjee,Jeremy Carleton,Sushil Vemuri,Srinivas Shakkottai,Dileep Kalathil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeHCA将事后指导的探索与事后信用分配相耦合，通过构建推理与工具交互树并推导逆向事后重要性修正，将终端结果传播为轮级优势，在七个问答基准上超过所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果监督的GRPO对LLM智能体轨迹中每个token赋予相同优势，无法区分有用与无用的决策；而事后信用分配（HCA）的估计依赖于轨迹在关键历史处包含有信息量的替代选项，二者均存在不足。

Method: TreeHCA利用参考答案似然的变化回访中间历史并采样替代续写，形成推理与工具交互树；针对该探索改变采样续写分布的问题，推导相应的逆向事后重要性修正（含精确有限池恒等式）；并在归一化树回传中利用该结构将终端结果传播为轮级优势。

Result: 在七个问答基准和三个Qwen骨干模型上，TreeHCA在总体精确匹配（exact match）指标上优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 共享的事后结构是TreeHCA的核心设计原则，将有信息量的探索与事后指导的信用分配相联结，验证了配对探索与信用分配对多轮LLM智能体训练的有效性。

Abstract: Outcome-supervised group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) assigns the same advantage to every token in an LLM agent trajectory, leaving useful and unhelpful decisions undifferentiated. Hindsight credit assignment (HCA) can refine this feedback, but its estimates rely on trajectories containing informative alternatives at consequential histories. We introduce TreeHCA, which couples hindsight-guided exploration with hindsight credit assignment. TreeHCA uses changes in reference-answer likelihood to revisit intermediate histories and sample alternative continuations, forming a tree of reasoning and tool interactions. Because this exploration changes the distribution of sampled continuations, we derive the corresponding inverse-hindsight importance correction, including an exact finite-pool identity. We use this structure in a normalized tree backup that propagates terminal outcomes into turn-level advantages. This shared hindsight structure is TreeHCA's central design principle, linking informative exploration with hindsight-guided credit assignment. Across seven question-answering benchmarks and three Qwen backbones, TreeHCA outperforms all baselines in aggregate exact match, validating the effectiveness of paired exploration and credit assignment for multi-turn LLM agent training. Our code is available at github.com/TheBlackCat22/TreeHCA.

</details>


### [45] [Cross-Behavior Policy Optimization: Unified Learning from Offline Demonstrations and On- and Off-Policy Rollouts via a Shared Regression Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12830)
*Du Cheng,Junyu Lu,Jingfan Zhang,Yuzhuo Zhang,Haoxuan Jia,Shichao Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Cross-Behavior Policy Optimization (CBPO)，用单一固定参考目标的回归损失统一离线示范与策略 rollout，实现 SFT 与 RL 目标的兼容与持续复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"先 SFT 后 RL"范式割裂了两者目标，导致已收集响应难以持续复用，且需额外协调多目标或引入行为策略概率、重要性权重与 critic。

Method: 通过固定参考目标构造统一回归损失，使生成策略不进入目标；正负离线补全、参考响应及当前/历史 rollout 共享统一"奖励标注记录"格式与回放缓冲区。按可用来源限制可导出离线、同策略、异策略变体，CBPO-Unified 为三者组合，无需存储行为策略概率、重要性加权或学习 critic。

Result: 在两种模型规模与四个数学推理基准上，CBPO 在各基准与数据设定下均取得领先的 avg@32 与 pass@32。Qwen3-1.7B 上，CBPO-Unified 相较 SFT DAPO 分别提升 2.33 与 4.44 个百分点；回放消融中保留历史 rollout 相比无历史配置提升 1.87 与 3.72 个百分点。

Conclusion: CBPO 以统一回归目标整合多源数据并支持持续回放复用，验证了历史 rollout 保留的有效性，为推理后训练提供简洁一致、性能领先的方案。

Abstract: Reasoning post-training draws on offline demonstrations and policy-generated rollouts, yet conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning (RL) separates their objectives and limits continued reuse of collected responses. We propose \textbf{Cross-Behavior Policy Optimization} (CBPO), which makes these sources compatible with one regression loss through a fixed-reference target. Because the generating policy does not enter this target, positive and negative offline completions, reference responses, and current and historical rollouts can share a common reward-labeled record format and replay buffer. As new responses enter the buffer, optimization retains the same regression objective without coordinating separate SFT and RL objectives or requiring stored behavior-policy probabilities, importance weighting, or a learned critic. Restricting the available sources gives offline, on-policy, and off-policy variants, while CBPO-Unified combines them. Across two model scales and four mathematical reasoning benchmarks, CBPO delivers leading \texttt{avg@32} and \texttt{pass@32} performance across benchmarks and data regimes. On Qwen3-1.7B, CBPO-Unified improves benchmark-averaged \texttt{avg@32} and \texttt{pass@32} over SFT DAPO by 2.33 and 4.44 percentage points, respectively. In the replay ablation, retaining historical rollouts improves these metrics by 1.87 and 3.72 percentage points over the no-history configuration.

</details>


### [46] [When Generative Models Act as Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12847)
*Sevan Brodjian,Michael Hobley,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生成模型能否作为模拟器使用，提出"可编辑性"（editability）这一概念：改变模型隐表示能否产生与编辑模拟器状态相同的下一步输出。作者在Othello棋局与自建Rayworld环境上对比了探针解码-引导和逆映射两种方法，发现何种映射成功高度依赖环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟器以用户可读、可改的状态概括环境，而生成模型虽能从观测中学习世界表示，却缺乏内置的世界状态编辑机制。作者希望探究生成模型在何种条件下可作为模拟器，即在仅有观测、映射后学的前提下，寻找其隐状态与模拟器状态之间的对应关系。

Method: 训练时仅使用观测数据，映射关系事后学习，测试两类映射：（1）训练探针从模型解码模拟器状态并沿其方向进行steering引导；（2）用逆映射直接学习该对应关系。实验在两个对比环境进行：离散棋盘游戏Othello、以及自建的连续运动圆盘世界Rayworld（通过部分渲染呈现，更接近物理场景与视频）。

Result: 每种变体中状态均可被解码，但映射成功与否强烈依赖环境。Othello中可编辑性随游戏简化而平滑下降，微小规则改动会带来巨大影响；Rayworld中探针无法沿着模拟器连续状态进行引导，而逆映射却能成功将模型置于该状态。

Conclusion: 生成模型可以被一个它可能从未学习过的模拟器所驱动。可编辑性的可达性取决于环境特性，逆映射在探针引导失效的情形下仍可能成功。

Abstract: A simulator summarizes an environment in a state that users can read and change. Generative models learn representations of the world without explicit specification, but offer no built-in way to edit that world's state. We investigate the conditions under which a generative model can act as a simulator through $\textit{editability}$: whether changing a model's latent representation produces the next-step outputs we get by editing a simulator's state. Plainly stated, our goal is to find a mapping between a generative model's latent state and the simulator state, where the model is trained on observations alone and the mapping is learned afterward. We test two kinds of mappings, both learned from observations paired with simulator states. In one, we train probes to decode the simulator state from the model, then steer along them. In the other, we learn the mapping directly with an inverse map, which can succeed even where steering fails. We study both in two contrasting environments: the discrete board game Othello, and Rayworld, our own continuous world of moving discs seen through partial renderings, closer to physical scenes and video. We find that which mapping succeeds depends strongly on the environment, even though the state is decodable in every variant. In Othello, editability falls smoothly as the game is simplified, and even small rule changes have large consequences. In Rayworld, probes fail to steer along the simulator's continuous state, yet an inverse map sets the model from that state anyway. A model can therefore be driven by a simulator it may have never learned.

</details>


### [47] [Reinforcement Learning via Fixed-point Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12848)
*Denis Blessing,Onur Celik,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出定点流匹配（Fixed-point Flow Matching, FFM），一种无需训练数据的流策略训练与微调方法，通过定点迭代形式化actor更新，使最优策略成为该迭代的不动点，并在离线到在线及在线RL中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大熵与行为约束强化学习中的最优策略服从Boltzmann分布，这种分布通常复杂且多模态。流策略结合流匹配能很好地表征此类分布，但传统流匹配依赖训练数据，而在线RL中通常无法获得这类数据。因此需要一种无需数据的流策略训练方法。

Method: 提出定点流匹配（FFM）：利用目标流恒等式将actor更新表述为定点迭代，采用流匹配风格的回归目标，以critic的动作梯度作为回归目标，从而在无数据条件下训练和微调流策略。理论上证明最优策略是该定点迭代的不动点。

Result: 大量实验表明，FFM能够在离线到在线（offline-to-online）设定下有效微调预训练流策略，并能在在线RL中从零开始高效且准确地训练流策略。

Conclusion: 定点流匹配是一种有原则的方法，可在无需训练数据的条件下学习具有表达力的基于流的策略。

Abstract: In maximum entropy and behavior-constrained reinforcement learning (RL), the optimal policy is given by a Boltzmann distribution. Generating samples from this distribution can be challenging, as it is often complex and multimodal. While flow policies trained with flow matching provide a powerful framework for representing such distributions, they traditionally rely on access to training data, which is generally unavailable in online RL. To address this limitation, we propose fixed-point flow matching (FFM), a data-free procedure for training and fine-tuning flow policies in RL. FFM leverages the target flow identity to formulate the actor update as a fixed-point iteration, utilizing a flow-matching-style regression objective where the critic's action gradients serve as regression targets. We show that the optimal policy is a fixed point of this iteration. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that FFM effectively fine-tunes pretrained flow policies in the offline-to-online setting and enables efficient and accurate training of flow policies from scratch in online RL. These results establish fixed-point flow matching as a principled approach for learning expressive flow-based policies without requiring training data.

</details>


### [48] [Robust Langevin Sampling under State-Dependent Heavy-Tailed Noise with Applications to LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12865)
*M. Berk Sahin,Ege C. Kaya,Arda Fazla,Behzad Sharif,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文放宽非对数凹采样中SG-LMC的两项强假设（全局Lipschitz梯度与一致有界噪声方差），提出广义光滑性与状态依赖重尾oracle，并给出R-LMC、A-LMC、RA-LMC三种方法及Fisher信息下的非渐近收敛保证，在合成与LLM微调实验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非对数凹采样的SG-LMC收敛保证通常要求梯度全局Lipschitz且噪声方差一致有界，但这两个假设在基础采样问题中即可能失效，放宽后更贴合如大语言模型贝叶斯微调等实际应用。

Method: 通过广义光滑性放宽Lipschitz梯度假设，提出具有限p阶矩（1<p≤2）的状态依赖重尾oracle以放宽方差有界假设；该oracle允许p<2时方差无穷并涵盖BG-0条件。分别与联合处理两类放宽，提出三种方法：R-LMC（几何中位数中心化与残差裁剪）、A-LMC（精确梯度下控制广义光滑性的离散化误差）、RA-LMC（稳健估计与噪声感知步长）。

Result: 三种方法均在Fisher信息意义下建立非渐近收敛保证，且不依赖耗散性或函数不等式。合成实验展示了FI收敛及各机制的作用；LLM与视觉-语言模型的贝叶斯微调实验支持所提oracle模型，RA-LMC在准确率与不确定性量化上匹配或超越SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 放宽梯度与噪声假设可提升非对数凹采样方法的实用性，所提R-LMC/A-LMC/RA-LMC在理论与实证上均具竞争力，为LLM贝叶斯微调等场景提供稳健采样方案。

Abstract: Existing convergence guarantees for non-log-concave sampling with stochastic gradient Langevin Monte Carlo (SG-LMC) typically assume globally Lipschitz gradients and uniformly bounded noise variance. Both assumptions can fail even in elementary sampling problems, and relaxing them better reflects practical applications such as Bayesian fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). We relax both assumptions through generalized smoothness and our proposed state-dependent heavy-tailed oracle with finite $p$-th moments, $1<p\leq 2$. The oracle permits infinite variance when $p<2$ and includes the Blum-Gladyshev (BG-0) condition. We study these relaxations separately and jointly, proposing three methods. Under globally Lipschitz gradients, robust LMC (R-LMC) handles the proposed oracle through geometric-median centering and residual clipping. With exact gradients, adaptive LMC (A-LMC) controls discretization error under generalized smoothness. Robust adaptive LMC (RA-LMC) addresses both relaxations through robust estimation and a noise-aware step size. For all three methods, we establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees in Fisher information (FI) without assuming dissipativity or a functional inequality. Synthetic experiments demonstrate FI convergence and the benefit of each mechanism. Bayesian fine-tuning experiments with LLMs and vision-language models support our oracle model and show that RA-LMC matches or outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [49] [HART: Hybrid Attention-RWKV Time series Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12883)
*Qingwen Lin,Boyan Xu,Shifeng Xie,Zhifeng Hao,Keli Zhang,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究非自回归多步预测中RWKV-7的长时程退化问题，提出PID机制解释该现象，并提出混合注意力-RWKV时序基础模型HART。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RWKV-7虽具强序列建模能力，但在非自回归多步预测设置下（未来patch作为缺失输入，循环状态需在无新观测情况下跨越预测时程演化）表现出长时程退化。作者希望理解该退化的机制并加以解决。

Method: 通过分析RWKV-7的广义delta规则，提出Placeholder Idling Decay (PID)机制：当占位符驱动的写入补偿不足时，反复的状态转移会衰减历史信息。层间分析显示多数层的状态注入范数下降，而转移矩阵范数在历史区与预测区相近，与PID一致。基于此提出HART，交替堆叠注意力块与RWKV块，同时结合对历史token的直接访问与循环时序建模。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval和TIME-Benchmark上的受控实验表明，HART在中长时程上优于纯RWKV，短时程性能相当，且总体表现优于同等规模的纯Transformer。HART以36M参数在GIFT-Eval长时程MASE上达到0.752，接近Toto-2.0-1B的0.754，参数量约为其1/28。

Conclusion: PID机制为RWKV-7在非自回归多步预测中的长时程退化提供了合理解释；交替注意力与RWKV架构的HART在保持短时程性能的同时显著改善长时程预测，并在极小参数量下取得与大模型相当的预测精度。

Abstract: Non-autoregressive multi-step forecasting (NAF) enables time series foundation models to predict an entire future horizon in a single forward pass. Despite its strong sequence modeling capabilities, RWKV-7 exhibits long-horizon degradation in our NAF experiments. In this setup, future patches are represented as missing inputs, requiring the recurrent state to evolve across the prediction horizon without new observations. Our analysis of RWKV-7's generalized delta rule suggests a mechanism we term Placeholder Idling Decay (PID): repeated state transitions can attenuate historical information when placeholder-driven writes provide insufficient compensation. Layer-wise analysis reveals reduced state-injection norms in most layers, while transition-matrix norms remain similar between historical and prediction regions, consistent with PID. Motivated by this analysis, we propose HART, a Hybrid Attention-RWKV Time-series Foundation Model that alternates attention and RWKV blocks, combining direct access to historical tokens with recurrent temporal modeling. Controlled experiments on GIFT-Eval and TIME-Benchmark show that HART improves over pure RWKV at medium and long horizons while maintaining comparable short-horizon performance, and achieves better aggregate performance than a comparably sized pure Transformer. With 36M parameters, HART achieves long-horizon MASE comparable to Toto-2.0-1B on GIFT-Eval (0.752 vs. 0.754), using approximately 1/28 of its parameters.

</details>


### [50] [CoGameReg: Cooperative-Game-Based Collaborative Semi-Supervised Regression for Seismic Reservoir Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12884)
*Zhiyu Guo,Jiwei Jia,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoGameReg是一个基于合作博弈的半监督回归框架，利用贡献加权构建共享伪标签，在稀疏井标签下融合异构回归器实现储层参数高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当地震数据（三维观测）丰富而井讲标签稀疏时，从地震数据准确估计储层属性仍然困难。现有方法难以在稀疏井控制下有效利用大量无标签地震样本，且异构回归器在伪标签生成中的贡献不明确。

Method: 提出CoGameReg半监督回归框架：使用异构回归器的贡献加权预测构建共享伪标签，使各回归器在无标签样本学习目标中的影响显式化；采用Tri-Game Shapley机制，结合rank-MSE性能博弈与成对一致性博弈，从有标签训练数据估计非负回归器权重，权重在协同训练中固定并复用于最终预测融合；候选伪标签在重训练前通过共同的局部k近邻误差缩减准则筛选。

Result: 在10个公开回归数据集、一个合成三维P波速度预测任务及一个实际含水饱和度应用中评估。基准优势在2.5%标签比例时最显著，随监督增加而减弱；合成实验获得最佳盲井指标；实际应用中预测截断至0-100%后，三口盲井汇总决定系数为0.5942，均方根误差为5.7441个百分点，Pearson相关为0.8265，四种方法中Pearson相关最高。

Conclusion: 结果支持使用CoGameReg整合稀疏井标签与密集地震属性，但性能在不同指标和井位间存在差异。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of reservoir properties from seismic data remains challenging when well-log labels are sparse but three-dimensional seismic observations are abundant. We present CoGameReg, a cooperative-game-based semi-supervised regression framework that uses contribution-weighted predictions of heterogeneous regressors to construct shared pseudo-labels. This design makes each regressor's influence explicit in the learning targets generated for unlabeled seismic samples under sparse well control. The Tri-Game Shapley mechanism combines a rank-MSE performance game with a pairwise consistency game to estimate nonnegative regressor weights from labeled training data. These weights remain fixed during collaborative training and are reused for final prediction fusion. Candidate pseudo-labels are screened using a common local k-nearest-neighbor error-reduction criterion before retraining. We evaluate CoGameReg on 10 public regression datasets, a synthetic three-dimensional P-wave velocity prediction task, and a field water-saturation application. The benchmark advantage is most pronounced at the 2.5% labeled-data ratio and decreases as supervision increases. CoGameReg obtains the best blind-well metrics in the synthetic experiment. In the field application, predictions clipped to 0-100% yield a pooled coefficient of determination of 0.5942, a root mean square error of 5.7441 percentage points, and a Pearson correlation of 0.8265 across three blind wells. CoGameReg has the highest Pearson correlation among the four field methods. The results support the use of CoGameReg to integrate sparse well labels with dense seismic attributes, with performance varying across metrics and well locations.

</details>


### [51] [Personalized Federated Direct Preference Optimization via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12885)
*Zhanhong Jiang,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出个性化联邦直接偏好优化 pFedDPO，用共享基础模型加本地 LoRA 适配器分解异构偏好，理论上将梯度多样性替换为更小的有效残差异构性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FedDPO 分析假设所有客户端收敛到单一共享策略，这与实际中异构的偏好分布相矛盾，无法刻画客户端个性化偏好。

Method: pFedDPO 中每个客户端在共享全局基础模型上维护小型私有 LoRA 适配器；每轮基础模型通过 FedAvg 同步，LoRA 适配器保留在本地以捕获客户端特有偏好模式。

Result: 理论证明该分解将 FedDPO 界中的梯度多样性 κ² 替换为有效残差异构性 κ²_eff，当适配器吸收客户端特异方向时 κ²_eff ≤ κ²，代价是随适配器收敛而消失的加性适配器偏置项；实验显示 pFedDPO 一致取得比 vanilla FedDPO 更低的梯度范数，同时在训练损失和每客户端奖励准确率上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过基础模型共享与本地 LoRA 适配器分解，pFedDPO 在保持竞争性性能的同时缓解了异构偏好导致的梯度多样性问题，为隐私保护下的个性化对齐提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Federated Direct Preference Optimization (FedDPO) enables privacy-preserving alignment of language models across distributed agents without centralizing sensitive preference data. However, existing FedDPO analysis assumes all clients converge to a single shared policy, which is fundamentally at odds with the heterogeneous preference distributions present in practice. We propose Personalized FedDPO (pFedDPO), in which each client maintains a small private LoRA adapter atop a shared global base model. In pFedDPO, the base model is synchronized via FedAvg each round, while LoRA adapters remain local and capture client-specific preference patterns. We prove that this decomposition replaces the gradient diversity $κ^2$ in the FedDPO bound with a residual heterogeneity $κ^2_{\mathrm{eff}}$, which is no larger than $κ^2$ when adapters absorb client-specific directions, at the cost of an additive adapter-bias term that vanishes as adapters converge. Experiments on preference datasets show that pFedDPO consistently attains a lower gradient norm than vanilla FedDPO while remaining competitive in training loss and per-client reward accuracy.

</details>

*Zhangyang Peng,Jingze Shi,Tongyi Zhang,Yuyu Luo*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于先验数据拟合网络的表格基础模型（TFM）在预训练中通常只用采样得到的硬查询标签（hard query labels），这引入了本可避免的采样变异性。作者观察到生成器的中间状态能够揭示超出单个采样结果的标签不确定性，因此希望利用这一信息来更稳定地监督查询。

Method: 提出 Generator-state-Informed Supervision for TFMs（GIST）：联合设计任务生成与查询监督，利用生成器的中间状态在条件标签分布上对查询损失求平均；采用熵校准编码（entropy-calibrated encodings），在受支持的、依赖标签的分支上获得可计算且与输入一致的目标。理论上证明该平均保持总体目标，并在每个生成分布内、固定参数下不增大梯度协方差。

Result: 在匹配的预训练调度下，GIST 在 TabArena 与 TALENT 两个基准上、跨三种架构均改善了平均排名，且不改变模型架构与推理过程；在这些基准与架构上，GIST 在与对应硬标签对照的配对数据集级比较中胜出 62.1%。

Conclusion: 利用生成器中间状态对条件标签分布求平均，是一种可保持总体目标、降低梯度协方差且无需改动架构或推理的有效预训练监督方式，可稳定提升表格基础模型的性能。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) based on prior-data fitted networks typically use sampled hard query labels, leaving avoidable sampling variability in pretraining. A key observation is that intermediate generator states can reveal label uncertainty beyond a sampled outcome. We propose Generator-state-Informed Supervision for TFMs (GIST), which uses these states to average query losses over conditional label distributions. GIST jointly designs task generation and query supervision, using entropy-calibrated encodings to obtain tractable, input-consistent targets on supported label-dependent branches. We show that this averaging preserves the population objective and yields no larger gradient covariance at fixed parameters within each generating distribution. Under matched pretraining schedules, GIST improves mean rank across three architectures on both TabArena and TALENT without changing architecture or inference. Across these benchmarks and architectures, GIST wins 62.1% of paired dataset-level comparisons against the corresponding hard-label controls.

</details>

*Tianjiao Luo,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

TL;DR: 本文从连续时间动力系统视角分析极小极大优化的不稳定性，将其建模为二阶ODE，指出损失函数Hessian在平衡点附近的特征结构是发散与极限环的根源，并提出Controlled-Minimax稳定化框架，通过反馈控制器实现局部指数稳定，在多个病态基准及高分辨率图像合成任务上有效抑制振荡并保证快速鲁棒收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小极大优化是对抗性学习的基础，但存在严重的稳定性问题，常表现为持续极限环或发散。传统梯度下降-上升等方法在一般极小极大问题上失效，现有启发式正则化技术缺乏理论保证。作者希望从根本上理解非收敛的成因，并给出具有理论保证的稳定化方案。

Method: 将训练动力学建模为二阶常微分方程（ODE），通过该连续时间动力系统框架严格识别非收敛的根源——平衡点附近损失函数Hessian的病态特征结构。在此基础上提出Controlled-Minimax框架，把优化轨迹重新表述为可控物理系统，并推导出特定的反馈控制器，从而在理论上保证局部指数稳定性，且不依赖底层Hessian谱的具体形态。

Result: 在多种病态基准上进行了实验，包括Dirac-GAN、二维高斯混合以及Swiss Roll数据集，以及基于StyleGAN2-ada的高分辨率图像合成任务。结果表明，Controlled-Minimax能有效抑制振荡行为，同时保证快速且鲁棒的收敛。

Conclusion: 通过连续时间动力系统分析揭示了极小极大优化不稳定的Hessian特征结构根源，并提出具有局部指数稳定理论保证的Controlled-Minimax框架，能够稳定传统梯度下降-上升方法失效的一般极小极大问题，在合成与真实高分辨率生成任务上均表现优异。

Abstract: Minimax optimization is fundamental to adversarial learning yet suffers from severe stability challenges, often manifesting as persistent limit cycles or divergence. This paper investigates these instabilities through the lens of continuous-time dynamical systems. By modeling training dynamics as a second-order Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE), we rigorously identify the root cause of non-convergence: the problematic eigenstructure of the loss function's Hessian near equilibrium points. To address this, we introduce Controlled-Minimax, a novel stabilization framework that reformulates the optimization trajectory as a controllable physical system. Unlike heuristic regularization techniques, our approach derives a specific feedback controller that provides theoretical guarantees of local exponential stability, regardless of the underlying Hessian spectrum. Consequently, our method stabilizes general minimax problems where traditional gradient descent-ascent methods fail. Extensive experiments on pathological benchmarks including the Dirac-GAN, 2D Gaussian mixtures, and Swiss Roll datasets, as well as high-resolution image synthesis with StyleGAN2-ada, demonstrate that Controlled-Minimax effectively suppresses oscillatory behavior while ensuring fast and robust convergence.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究在效用变化后的匹配修复问题，提出残差负载修复（RLR）方法，结合可靠结果证书与批量匹配调度，并给出紧致的发布轮次界、延迟与证据生命周期界，以及信息负载下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配修复可重用有效观测，但新评估须受匹配容量约束且可能乱序返回，因此需要在公告式失效、有界且可能依赖奖励的报告延迟，以及不占用未来匹配容量的待定评估条件下，设计可证明有效的修复机制。

Method: 提出残差负载修复（RLR）：将可靠结果证书与面向不等样本缺口的批量匹配调度结合；采用完整二分配额调度；并提出RLR-P，在不改变采样计划的前提下监控完整发布顺序前缀；使用确定性纯oracle与单次修复耦合分析；同时推导信息负载下界，并针对固定优先级星形市场与未知Bernoulli差距分析PrioritySearch。

Result: RLR的完整二分配额调度获得相对终端残差端点负载系数cb<1.607的紧致发布轮次界，并给出独立的延迟界与证据生命周期界；在相同耗尽初始状态下，单次修复耦合保证认证时间、已发布观测数或到达可重用检查点的时间不增加；在固定优先级星形市场、未知Bernoulli差距下，PrioritySearch在δ→0时达到历史调整的一阶常数，且满足与假设无关的线性历史缩放和次对数报告延迟；配对仿真与实测反馈回放显示RLR-P的平均截断更新认证时间低于基于监控宽度的基线，但oracle CPU成本更高。

Conclusion: RLR与RLR-P在带延迟与容量约束的匹配修复中提供了可证明的性能保证，并在仿真与回放中表现出认证时间优势，但以更高的oracle计算成本为代价。

Abstract: Matching repair after utility changes can reuse valid observations, but new evaluations must respect matching capacity and may return out of order. We study announced invalidations with bounded, possibly reward-dependent reporting delays and pending evaluations that do not occupy future matching capacity. Residualload repair (RLR) combines sound outcome certificates with batched matching schedules for unequal sample deficits. Its complete dyadic quota schedule admits a sharp issue-round bound with coefficient cb < 1.607 relative to the terminal residual endpoint load, together with separate delay and evidence-lifetime bounds. RLR-P monitors complete issue-order prefixes without changing the sampling plan before acceptance. With a deterministic pure oracle, a single-repair coupling from the same drained initial state guarantees no increase in certification time, issued observations, or time to a reusable checkpoint. We also derive an information-load lower bound. On fixed priority-star markets with unknown Bernoulli gaps, PrioritySearch attains the history-adjusted first-order constant for expected certification time as δ {\sim} 0, under hypothesis-independent linear history scaling and sublogarithmic reporting delays. Paired simulations and measured-feedback replay quantify the trade-offs: in the replay, RLR-P has lower mean capped update-certification time than a monitored width-based baseline, at higher oracle CPU cost.

</details>


### [55] [Diffusion Precipitation Generation with Medium-range Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12976)
*Guowen Li,Yang Liu,Mengxuan Chen,Jiashun Cheng,Haoyuan Liang,Shilei Cao,Yujie Wang,Juepeng Zheng,Fang Wang,Yerong Feng,Meng Jin,Hong Cheng,Yutong Lu,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dynamics-Precipitation Decoupling 框架，用独立训练的扩散生成器将冻结气象模型的非降水预报映射为与观测对齐的日降水，提升日降水预报精度与事件检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前天气基础模型多基于再分析数据训练，存在区域与强度相关的偏差；直接在观测目标上重训成本高昂，因此需要一种即插即用的方案来生成观测对齐的日降水预报。

Method: 该框架解耦大气动力与降水生成：以冻结气象模型的非降水预报为输入，训练条件扩散生成器，条件为大气状态与卫星-雨量计降水估计。多尺度循环编码器汇总大气演变，交叉注意力在去噪过程中对齐大气与降水特征。

Result: 在 Pangu-Weather、GraphCast、HRES 等骨干及全球、区域预报上评估，日降水精度和事件检测均有提升，局部累积分布表征更好；全球集合评估在所有报告时效上 Brier 分数低于 ENS 与从头训练的 GenCast，多数对比中 CRPS 更低；中国 2288 站强降水案例研究进一步验证了累积格局与事件检测的改进。

Conclusion: 通过解耦动力学与降水并采用条件扩散生成，可在不重训大型天气模型的前提下有效获得观测对齐的日降水预报，具备跨骨干、跨区域的泛化性与业务应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate daily precipitation forecasts are important for weather-sensitive decisions. Although recent Weather Foundation Models (WFMs) have achieved significant progress, they are trained on reanalysis data, which exhibit regional and intensity-dependent biases. Since retraining WFMs on observation-based targets is costly, we study Dynamics-Precipitation Decoupling, a plug-and-play framework that uses a separately trained generator to map non-precipitation forecasts from a frozen weather model to observation-aligned daily precipitation. Its key challenges are to capture heterogeneous rainfall distributions and align daily accumulations with atmospheric signals that evolve across space and time. To address them, we train a diffusion generator conditional on atmospheric states and satellite-gauge estimates of precipitation. A multi-scale recurrent encoder summarizes atmospheric evolution, while cross-attention aligns atmospheric and precipitation features during denoising. Extensive evaluation of various backbones, including Pangu-Weather, GraphCast, and HRES, on global and regional forecasting shows improved daily precipitation accuracy and event detection, with better representation of localized accumulation patterns. Global ensemble evaluation shows lower Brier scores than ENS and training-from-scratch GenCast at all reported lead times, with lower CRPS in most comparisons. Remarkably, a heavy-rainfall case study using 2,288 stations in China further supports improved daily accumulation patterns and event detection against ground observations.

</details>


### [56] [Gaussian-Width Bounds for Offline-to-Online Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12990)
*Kushagra Chandak,Volodymyr Tkachuk,Xiaoqi Tan,Csaba Szepesvári*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究离线数据（部分覆盖）如何辅助随机线性赌博机在线学习，通过更紧的奖励预测误差控制将遗憾界从 Õ(√dkT) 改进至 Õ(k√T)，并给出匹配下界及纯探索问题的算法与界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机线性赌博机中，离线数据可辅助在线学习。实际场景中离线数据常为"部分覆盖"，即 d 个特征方向中只有 k 个覆盖不足。已有基于分阶段消除与乐观策略的分析给出 Õ(√dkT) 遗憾界，作者认为该界源于对奖励预测误差的松弛控制，因此希望获得更紧的界。

Method: 利用高斯向量 Lipschitz 函数的集中不等式，对奖励预测误差进行更紧的控制；据此设计分阶段消除（phased elimination）算法。同时针对单位欧氏球，在部分覆盖下证明匹配的遗憾下界，在均匀离线覆盖下给出匹配的上下界。对纯探索问题，分别针对固定设计与自适应算法，在部分覆盖与均匀覆盖下给出算法及匹配的简单遗憾上下界。

Result: 在部分覆盖下，所提分阶段消除算法的遗憾界为 Õ(k√T)，优于既有 Õ(√dkT)，并在单位欧氏球上证明匹配下界；均匀离线覆盖下上下界匹配。纯探索方面，给出固定设计与自适应算法在部分覆盖、均匀覆盖下的匹配简单遗憾上下界。

Conclusion: 紧的奖励预测误差控制是改进离线辅助在线学习遗憾界的关键；所提算法与下界在部分覆盖与均匀覆盖两类设定下均达到最优或匹配，且结论可推广至带离线数据的纯探索问题。

Abstract: We study how offline data can help online learning in stochastic linear bandits. We focus on partially covered offline data, where only $k$ of the $d$ feature directions are poorly covered. Existing analyses of algorithms based on phased elimination and optimism give regret bounds of order $\widetilde O(\sqrt{dkT})$ in this regime. We show that this dependence arises from loose control of the reward prediction error, and obtain tighter bounds using concentration of Lipschitz functions of Gaussian vectors. Based on this analysis, we develop a phased elimination algorithm with regret $\widetilde O(k\sqrt T)$. For the unit Euclidean ball, we prove a matching regret lower bound under partial coverage, as well as matching upper and lower bounds under uniform offline coverage. We also study pure exploration with offline data, providing algorithms and matching upper and lower bounds on simple regret for fixed-design algorithms and also for adaptive algorithms under partial and uniform coverage of offline data.

</details>


### [57] [Evidential Multimodal Transformers with Dempster-Shafer Fusion for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13013)
*Muhammad Usama,Yeoreum Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EMT-DS（证据多模态Transformer+Dempster-Shafer融合），用主观意见与冲突系数显式量化多模态情感识别中的认知/偶然不确定性，在四个基准上提升准确率与校准性并鲁棒应对噪声与模态缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Softmax的多模态情感识别在传感器损坏或模态矛盾（如面部表情呈正向效价而语音韵律呈负向唤醒）时仍给出高置信预测，缺乏形式化不确定性量化，导致过度自信与无依据的共识。

Method: EMT-DS将预测建模为主观意见（Dirichlet分布），分离认知不确定性（证据不足）与偶然不确定性（传感器噪声）；各模态经专用Transformer编码器输出信念质量分布，再用Dempster组合规则融合，以冲突系数1/(1-c)归一化联合信念质量，使矛盾证据被显式量化为冲突并产生弥散化预测而非强行共识；融合复杂度为O(MK^2)，低于跨模态注意力的O(M^2 d^2)。

Result: 在主体独立与说话人独立评估下：IEMOCAP 84.3% vs 最佳基线81.7%（p<0.01）；DEAP留一被试82.4% vs 78.1%；高度不平衡KEMDy20达96.8%；CMU-MOSEI较最强证据基线提升2.4个百分点。消融中ECE由0.153降至0.042；音频特征严重高斯噪声（sigma=1.0）下保持71.2%，注意力基线降至45.3%；整模态缺失时性能平缓退化。冲突度量可标记错误：审查20%样本可覆盖80%误分类。

Conclusion: EMT-DS通过证据理论与Dempster-Shafer融合实现多模态情感识别的形式化不确定性量化，兼顾精度、校准、鲁棒性与冲突感知的可选择性预测，适用于临床与安全关键场景。

Abstract: Softmax-based multimodal emotion recognition methods often assign high confidence even when sensor data are corrupted or contradictory, for example when facial expressions suggest positive valence while vocal prosody indicates negative arousal. We present an Evidential Multimodal Transformer with Dempster-Shafer Fusion (EMT-DS) that addresses this problem through formal uncertainty quantification. EMT-DS models predictions as Subjective Opinions (Dirichlet distributions), separating epistemic uncertainty (lack of evidence) from aleatoric uncertainty (sensor noise). Modality-specific transformer encoders produce belief mass distributions that are fused with Dempster's Rule of Combination, which normalizes the joint belief mass by a conflict coefficient 1/(1-c), so that contradictory evidence is explicitly quantified as conflict and the fused prediction becomes diffuse rather than being forced into an unjustified consensus. Under subject-independent and speaker-independent evaluation on four benchmarks, EMT-DS achieves 84.3% accuracy on IEMOCAP versus 81.7% for the best baseline (p < 0.01), 82.4% on DEAP under leave-one-subject-out evaluation versus 78.1%, and 96.8% on the highly imbalanced KEMDy20, and it improves accuracy on CMU-MOSEI by 2.4 points over the strongest evidential baseline. In controlled ablation, it reduces Expected Calibration Error from 0.153 (softmax transformer) to 0.042. It maintains 71.2% accuracy under severe Gaussian noise on audio features (sigma = 1.0), where attention baselines drop to 45.3%, and degrades gracefully when entire modalities are absent. The fusion step has O(MK^2) complexity, compared with O(M^2 d^2) for cross-modal attention. The learned conflict metric also flags likely errors: reviewing 20% of samples captures 80% of misclassifications, which supports selective prediction in clinical and safety-critical systems.

</details>


### [58] [Generalized Proximal Policy Optimization: Unifying LLM RL Algorithms from the Perspective of Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13015)
*Zhicheng Cai,Xinyuan Guo,Hanlin Wu,Weinan Dai,Yuyan Zhou,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GPPO统一框架，从散度视角重构LLM强化学习算法设计，建立广义f-散度信任域理论，将PPO、DAPO、DPPO、RIPO视为特例，并在数学与代码任务上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流PPO采用几何退化的裁剪代理，无法自适应地调节对策略偏移的抵抗，导致过早裁剪与探索退化；后续变体多为碎片化、启发式修补，缺乏统一原理性理解。

Method: 提出广义PPO（GPPO）：建立带单调性能提升保证的广义f-散度信任域理论，揭示不同f-散度诱导不同几何与策略更新动态；引入新的估计器拓扑，比传统单样本蒙特卡洛估计更忠实；形成{散度×估计器}二维设计空间。

Result: 在8个竞赛级数学与代码基准上，两个维度均关键；最优GPPO实例在数学与代码上分别超越标准GRPO最高达50%与14%，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: GPPO为LLM强化学习提供原理性、统一的算法设计框架，PPO及DAPO、DPPO、RIPO等为特例，验证了散度与估计器联合设计的重要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become the foundational paradigm for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Yet the dominant approach, Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), relies on a geometrically degenerate clipping proxy that cannot adapt its resistance to policy shifts, leading to premature clipping and degraded exploration. Subsequent variants alleviate these issues through fragmented and heuristic modifications. We propose Generalized Proximal Policy Optimization (GPPO), a principled and unified framework for understanding and designing LLM RL algorithms through the lens of divergence. We establish a generalized $f$-divergence trust-region theory with a monotonic performance improvement guarantee, and show that different $f$-divergences induce distinct geometries and policy update dynamics. We further introduce novel estimator topologies that provide more faithful divergence estimates than the conventional single-sample Monte Carlo estimation. Under this framework, PPO and recent variants such as DAPO, DPPO, and RIPO emerge as special cases, establishing a principled two-dimensional design space of {Divergence $\times$ Estimator}. Extensive experiments on eight competition-level math and coding benchmarks show that both dimensions are critical. The best-performing GPPO instantiation surpasses standard GRPO by up to 50% and 14% on math and coding, delivering SOTA performance.

</details>


### [59] [Trajectory-Aware Policy Optimization: Closing the State Distribution Gap in Long-Horizon LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13020)
*Zhicheng Cai,Weinan Dai,Yuyan Zhou,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹感知策略优化（TAPO），从离策略策略梯度理论推导状态分布校正项并融入RL目标，解决长程自回归展开中的状态分布偏移问题，在八个竞赛级基准上显著超越GRPO等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为提升LLM推理能力的主流范式，但长程自回归展开存在根本局限：局部策略失配会沿轨迹累积成严重的状态分布偏移。现有方法（GRPO、GSPO）仅在token级或序列级控制策略失配，忽略了轨迹诱导的偏移，导致训练不稳定并随训练时长增长最终崩溃。

Method: 提出TAPO框架：从离策略策略梯度理论出发，理论推导出状态分布校正项，并将其纳入RL目标以弥合状态分布差距；同时采用几何均值估计器进行方差缩减，并引入长度感知的动态裁剪以阻止轨迹中过度偏离的更新。

Result: 在八个竞赛级基准上，TAPO显著超越现有LLM RL算法：在数学推理上较GRPO提升最高达48%，在算法编码上提升最高达12%，同时训练稳定性大幅增强。

Conclusion: 通过显式校正轨迹诱导的状态分布偏移，TAPO在理论上有据可依地解决了长程LLM RL训练的稳定性与性能瓶颈，验证了状态分布校正对长程策略优化的关键作用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant paradigm for enhancing LLMs' reasoning capabilities. Yet long-horizon autoregressive rollouts introduce a fundamental limitation: local policy mismatch can compound into severe state distribution shift over trajectories. Existing LLM RL methods, such as GRPO and GSPO, control policy mismatch at the token or sequence level, but overlook this trajectory-induced shift, causing training instability and eventual collapse as the horizon grows. To close the state distribution gap, we propose Trajectory-Aware Policy Optimization (TAPO), a principled framework that theoretically derives the state-distribution correction from the off-policy policy gradient and incorporates it into the RL objective. TAPO further employs a geometric-mean estimator for variance reduction, together with a length-aware dynamic clipping that prevents overly deviated updates across the trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TAPO significantly surpasses existing LLM RL algorithms across eight competition-level benchmarks, achieving up to 48% and 12% improvement over GRPO on mathematical reasoning and algorithmic coding, with substantially enhanced training stability.

</details>


### [60] [Low-Rank Kolmogorov-Arnold Generator Flows for Interpretable Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13028)
*Minjong Cheon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Kolmogorov-Arnold flow (KAFlow)，将物理算子生成元表示为基于单变量 Kolmogorov-Arnold 函数的低秩符号，配合规范固定、精确指数积分器与自动闭式蒸馏，在学习位置相关动力学时显著提升分布外泛化能力并自动恢复闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子应从数据中学习物理规律，既能泛化到训练分布之外，又能以闭式形式显式给出所学规律。现有 Kolmogorov-Arnold 神经算子虽能同时实现这两点，但其在所有位置与频率上学习稠密伪微分符号，计算代价随分辨率平方增长，且闭式形式需要手工读出，因此存在效率与可解释性上的不足。

Method: 作者首先论证稠密符号并非必要：有限阶变系数微分算子的符号，其分离秩被系数函数个数所界定，且与分辨率无关，而在该网络自身的基准上所学符号的秩仅为二。基于此提出 KAFlow：将物理算子生成元学习为由单变量 Kolmogorov-Arnold 函数构成的低秩符号；固定这些函数的规范以保证每一项具有唯一位置；用精确指数积分器对动力学进行积分；并自动蒸馏其闭式形式。

Result: 在 Kolmogorov-Arnold 神经算子的合成基准上，KAFlow 使分布外损失降低 3.4 至 11 倍，并在无符号模板的情况下将每个系数恢复到 10^-4 以内，达到数据的精度下限。在长时程量子动力学中，其状态不保真度最多降低 6 个数量级；仅用位置与动量测量训练时降低 30 倍。KAFlow 学习变阶分数阶算子的精度比现有算子网络高 15 倍，并能发现稀疏回归无法处理的非局部、分数阶及非多项式色散关系。

Conclusion: KAFlow 通过低秩符号表示与自动闭式蒸馏，在保持甚至提升精度的同时克服了稠密符号在计算代价和手工读出方面的限制，能够高精度恢复物理系数、提升长时程与分布外泛化性能，并发现稀疏回归难以捕捉的复杂色散关系，展现出学习可解释物理规律的有效性。

Abstract: Neural operators that learn physical laws from data should generalize beyond their training distribution and expose the learned law in closed form. The Kolmogorov-Arnold neural operator achieves both for position-dependent dynamics by learning a dense pseudo-differential symbol over all positions and frequencies, at a cost that grows with the square of the resolution and with closed forms that must be read out by hand. This dense symbol is unnecessary: a finite-order variable-coefficient differential operator has a symbol whose separation rank is bounded by the number of its coefficient functions, independently of the resolution, and the symbols that this network learns on its own benchmarks have rank two. We introduce the Kolmogorov-Arnold flow (KAFlow), which learns the generator of a physical operator as a low-rank symbol built from univariate Kolmogorov-Arnold functions, fixes the gauge of these functions so that every term has a unique place, integrates the dynamics with exact exponential integrators, and distills its closed form automatically. On the synthetic benchmarks of the Kolmogorov-Arnold neural operator, KAFlow lowers the out-of-distribution loss by 3.4 to 11 times and recovers every coefficient to within 10^-4 without symbolic templates, reaching the precision floor of the data. On long-horizon quantum dynamics, it lowers the state infidelity by up to 6 orders of magnitude, and by 30 times when trained only on position and momentum measurements. KAFlow also learns a variable-order fractional operator 15 times more accurately than existing operator networks and discovers nonlocal, fractional and non-polynomial dispersion relations on which sparse regression fails.

</details>


### [61] [Target-Constrained Pooling for Trajectory Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13034)
*Ning Wang,Claire Cardie,Sainyam Galhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出目标约束池化（TCP）方法，利用相关完整轨迹的信息提升部分目标轨迹的外推预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部分目标轨迹进行轨迹外推本质上是不确定的，但已有的相关完整轨迹可提供关于未解析后续走势的信息，因此需要一种方法挖掘这类辅助信息。

Method: TCP将有限时域端点与后续形状分离，并从两种互补源信号估计该端点：均匀池化捕获最终响应水平，匹配增量池化在对齐源与目标状态后捕获剩余变化。方法还筛选代表性源、将两个端点估计投影到目标一致区域、转化为对自由预测的修正，并通过强度校准与退化感知加权控制其影响。

Result: 在五个领域、双向预测的25,245个目标上，TCP使十种仅目标预测器的平均RMSE降低35.8%至42.1%，并在全部100组预测器、数据集与方向比较中均有提升；两种端点信号优于任一单独使用，代表性筛选在仅用12.7倍更少供体输入和138.8倍更少匹配距离计算的情况下，性能保持在完整供体池的0.64%至1.02%范围内。

Conclusion: TCP通过融合互补的端点估计与高效的代表性源选择，显著且稳定地改善了轨迹外推性能。

Abstract: Trajectory extrapolation from a partial target trajectory is underdetermined, but completed related trajectories provide information about the unresolved continuation. We propose target-constrained pooling (TCP), which separates the finite-horizon endpoint from continuation shape and estimates that endpoint from two complementary source signals. Uniform pooling captures final response levels, while matched-increment pooling captures the remaining change after aligning source and target states. TCP selects representative sources, projects both endpoint estimates onto a target-consistent region, converts them into corrections of the free forecast, and controls their influence through strength calibration and degradation-aware weighting. Across 25,245 targets from five domains and both forecasting directions, TCP reduces mean RMSE by 35.8% to 42.1% for all ten target-only forecasters and improves all 100 forecaster, dataset, and direction comparisons. The two endpoint signals consistently outperform either alone, while representative selection remains within 0.64% to 1.02% of the full donor pool using 12.7x fewer donor inputs and 138.8x fewer matching distance computations.

</details>


### [62] [Towards Easier-to-Tune Training Algorithms: New Results from AlgoPerf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13036)
*Priya Kasimbeg,Ahmed Khaled,Aahlad Chandrabhatta,Yifan Wang,Andy Liu,Niccolò Ajroldi,David Tweedle,Xingyu,Yang,Isaac Patole,Frank Schneider,George E. Dahl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了更新版AlgoPerf V1基准在自调优规则集下的结果：在固定硬件、九个工作负载、以墙钟时间到目标评价下，PyTorch与JAX共13个提交中Schedule-Free AdamW排名第一，并分析了步数效率与运行时间的权衡及超参数敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估深度学习优化器很困难：超参数调优、优化器-模型协同设计等混杂因素常掩盖真实算法性能。因此需要一个控制这些因素、在多工作负载和固定硬件上进行公平比较的基准。

Method: 使用更新版AlgoPerf V1基准，在固定硬件上对九个工作负载以墙钟时间到目标（time-to-target）评估一组新训练算法；重点采用自调优规则集，要求提交用一个统一配置跨所有工作负载；并开展步数效率与墙钟执行的权衡分析，以及超参数显著性与保留性分析。

Result: 13个PyTorch和JAX提交中，Schedule-Free AdamW排名第一；矩阵预条件与双环（two-loop）方法样本效率较好，但需更激进的计算优化才能在运行时间上有竞争力；学习率和动量在不同领域与算法间高度敏感。

Conclusion: 优化器评估应在受控条件下以墙钟时间为标准；当前方法在超参数鲁棒性和跨新任务泛化方面仍有不足，需发展对超参数调优更鲁棒、可泛化的优化方法。

Abstract: Evaluating deep learning optimizer is challenging because confounding factors such as hyperparameter tuning and optimizer-model co-design often obscure true algorithmic performance. We present results of the updated AlgoPerf V1 benchmark, in which we evaluate a new set of training algorithms on wall-clock time-to-target across nine workloads on fixed hardware. This report focuses on the results of the benchmark's self-tuning ruleset, which requires submissions to use a single configuration across all workloads. Out of the 13 submissions in PyTorch and JAX, Schedule-Free AdamW ranked first. In addition to the wall-clock scores, we also analyze the trade-offs between step efficiency and wall-clock execution, revealing that matrix-preconditioned and two-loop methods achieve good sample efficiency but require more aggressive computational optimizations to be competitive in runtime. Finally, our hyperparameter saliency and retention analyses show that the learning rate and momentum are highly sensitive across domains and algorithms, highlighting the need for methods that are more robust against hyperparameter tuning and can generalize well to new tasks.

</details>


### [63] [CB-Orchestrator: Adaptive Workflow Optimization for LLM Agents via Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13064)
*Jiahang Sun,Zhiwei Shang,Zhipiao Liu,Hongwei Yang,Guoqing Xie,Shuang Qiu,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CB-Orchestrator，将LLM智能体工作流优化解耦为“候选工作流生成”与“上下文赌博机选择”两阶段，在提升性能的同时大幅降低训练阶段API开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动设计LLM智能体工作流成本高、灵活性差；现有自动化优化方法在训练阶段收集反馈时API开销昂贵，且无法自适应复用高质量工作流，成为性能与成本效益的瓶颈。

Method: CB-Orchestrator将工作流生成与选择解耦：第一阶段通过进化搜索构建多样化的候选工作流池；第二阶段将工作流选择建模为上下文赌博机（Contextual Bandit）问题，通过探索-利用平衡实现样本高效学习，减少训练所需反馈；推理时针对每个查询自适应选择最优工作流。

Result: 在五个多样化基准上一致优于所有基线；在MATH数据集上，性能优于近期强基线，同时训练阶段API token开销降低不少于40.55%，端到端训练（含工作流池生成）token开销降低不少于29.73%。

Conclusion: 将工作流生成与选择解耦并结合上下文赌博机，可兼顾LLM智能体工作流的性能与成本效益，实现查询级自适应工作流复用。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in tackling complex tasks through agentic workflows. However, manually designing these workflows is labor-intensive and lacks flexibility to adapt to diverse queries. While automated workflow optimization is a promising direction, existing methods often incur expensive API costs during training-phase feedback collection. Furthermore, they fail to adaptively reuse high-quality workflows, which creates a bottleneck for both performance and cost-effectiveness. To address these limitations, we propose CB-Orchestrator, an adaptive framework that decouples workflow generation from selection. In the first stage, we construct a diverse pool of candidate workflows via evolutionary search. In the second stage, we formulate workflow selection as a Contextual Bandit problem, which enables sample-efficient learning by balancing exploration and exploitation, thereby significantly reducing the feedback required for training. During inference, the model adaptively selects the optimal workflow tailored to each query. Evaluations across five diverse benchmarks demonstrate that CB-Orchestrator consistently outperforms all baselines. Notably, on the MATH dataset, CB-Orchestrator achieves better performance than strong recent baselines while reducing training-phase API token overhead by no less than 40.55% and total end-to-end training (including workflow pool generation) token overhead by no less than 29.73%.

</details>


### [64] [Double-Take: Improving Capacity, Binding, and Selectivity In Long-Context Within Bidirectional Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13073)
*Vakul Raghavan,Utkarsh Grover,Mingyang Mao,Chengwen Wang,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究长序列下"可用记忆"的构建问题，指出容量、绑定与选择性是长程记忆的三个关键要求，并提出Double-Take（DT）方法修复大块测试时训练中的两类写入失败，显著提升跨位置检索与长程泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理长序列并不保证远距离信息仍可用。随着上下文增长，固定大小的记忆需同时保留大量关联、跨位置连接信息并维持相关结构，而不被冗余信息淹没。存储更多信息不等于能表示检索所需的关系，传递关系也不等于保留关键关系。因此核心挑战不是单纯延长上下文长度，而是构建随上下文增长仍保持可用的记忆。

Method: 在大块测试时训练（large-chunk TTT）框架下，论文识别出两类基本失败：单步写入会累积干扰、限制容量；自关联写入无法形成跨位置绑定。为此提出Double-Take（DT），采用多步无固定（unpinned）更新与跨位置混合来修复上述问题，并加入对变化敏感的双向写入以实现选择性保留。

Result: 仅提升容量时端到端召回停留在0.10的随机水平；加入绑定后提升至0.92。仅在32 kb基因组上下文上训练的DT，在131 kb上达到0.927 AUROC，在接近1 Mb时达到0.856，并能从人类零样本迁移至小鼠。

Conclusion: 结果表明，容量、绑定与选择性是构建可用长程记忆的三项基本要求；通过多步写入、跨位置混合与变化敏感的选择性保留可协同克服大块TTT中的写入失败，实现稳健的长程检索与跨物种零样本泛化。

Abstract: Processing a long sequence does not guarantee that distant information remains usable. As context grows, a fixed-size memory must retain many associations, connect information across positions, and preserve relevant structure without being overwhelmed by everything it observes. These are distinct requirements: a memory may store more information without representing the relationships needed for retrieval, and it may transport those relationships without preserving the ones that matter. Thus, the challenge is not simply extending context length, but constructing a memory that remains usable as context grows. We study this problem in large-chunk test-time training (TTT) and identify two basic failures: single-step writes accumulate interference, limiting capacity, while self-associative writes cannot form cross-position bindings. We introduce Double-Take (DT), which repairs these failures with multi-step unpinned updates and cross-position mixing, and adds bidirectional change-sensitive writes for selective retention. Capacity alone leaves end-to-end recall at the $0.10$ chance floor; adding binding raises it to $0.92$. Trained only on $32\,\mathrm{kb}$ genomic contexts, DT reaches $0.927$ AUROC at $131\,\mathrm{kb}$ and $0.856$ near $1\,\mathrm{Mb}$, while transferring zero-shot from human to mouse. Together, these results identify capacity, binding, and selectivity as three requirements for usable long-range memory.

</details>


### [65] [Human and LLM Facilitation in Collaborative Design Meetings: A Computational Behavioral Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13082)
*Mohammed Azeez Khan,Aaron Dsouza,Ashutosh Mishra,Ummul Faiz Z. Bibi,Neha K. Nair,Amar Kumar Behera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于AMI语料库199对配对语境的对比发现：人类PM在语境语义距离与模糊限制语密度上更高，Llama 3.1 8B在话题发散度、词汇扩展代理指标与阶段词汇对齐上更高；该差异仅描述行为画像，不构成促进质量的优劣评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于促进群体讨论与协作，但LLM促进与人类促进之间的行为差异尚未被充分刻画；研究需要一个可复用的行为基准，以支持后续对LLM促进者、人类促进者、提示、模型与协作情境的比较。

Method: 从AMI Meeting Corpus抽取基于场景的协作设计会议中人类项目经理（PM）的促进话轮，与Llama 3.1 8B Instruct的回复在199对配对语境上进行比较。分析包含五个验证性维度：语境语义距离、词汇扩展代理指标、共情与模糊限制语词汇密度、话题发散度、阶段词汇对齐；另有一项探索性指令性（directiveness）指标未通过领域效度检验，故不解读。并设置了无阶段（no-phase）二次检验。

Result: 人类PM话轮在语境语义距离（r = 0.644）与共情及模糊限制语密度（r = 0.203）上更高；后者主要由模糊限制语词汇驱动，故解释为词汇性模糊限制而非心理共情。Llama生成的促进在话题发散度（r = 0.703）、词汇扩展代理指标（r = 0.288，受长度混淆）以及受指令条件约束的阶段词汇对齐（r = 0.382）上更高。无阶段二次检验后，Llama更高的阶段对比依然成立，但系统提示仍要求一个促进动作，因此该检验不能作为自发性阶段意识的证据。

Conclusion: 人类与LLM促进者在多个可测量维度上呈现系统性的行为差异，但不应将这些差异解读为促进质量的排序。所得行为画像为后续LLM促进者、人类促进者、提示、模型与协作情境之间的比较提供了参考基点。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored as facilitators of group discussion and collaborative work, but the behavioral differences between LLM-generated and human facilitation remain insufficiently characterized. We compare human Project Manager facilitation turns from scenario-based collaborative design meetings with Llama 3.1 8B Instruct responses across 199 paired contexts from the AMI Meeting Corpus. The analysis covers five confirmatory dimensions: contextual semantic distance, a lexical elaboration proxy, empathyand-hedge lexical density, topic divergence, and phase-vocabulary alignment. An exploratory directiveness measure failed domain validation and is not interpreted. The sampled human Project Manager turns were higher on contextual semantic distance (r = 0.644) and on empathyand-hedge lexical density (r = 0.203). The second contrast is carried mainly by hedge terms, so we read it as lexical hedging rather than psychological empathy. Llama-generated facilitation was higher on topic divergence (r = 0.703), the lexical elaboration proxy (r = 0.288; lengthconfounded), and instruction-conditioned phase-vocabulary alignment (r = 0.382). A secondary no-phase check left the Llama-higher phase contrast in place. The system prompt still requested a facilitation move, so that check is not evidence of spontaneous phase awareness. These differences should not be interpreted as a ranking of facilitation quality. The resulting behavioral profile provides a reference point for later comparisons of LLM facilitators, human facilitators, prompts, models, and collaborative settings.

</details>


### [66] [Can Failed Trajectories Reveal Where Agents Go Wrong? Agentic Failure Attribution Without Step-Level Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13105)
*Do Nguyen-Thanh,Junlin Fang,Bing Li,Zhen Fang,Sean Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SOAP框架，在无步骤级标签、无成功参考轨迹的条件下，通过谱参考空间打分与注意力引导的错误传播，实现LLM智能体失败归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体失败归因对调试与改进智能体系统至关重要。现有方法依赖昂贵的LLM提示、步骤级错误标注，或需要已验证的成功轨迹提供参考，但成功轨迹本身缺乏失败信号。本文研究更具挑战的设定：仅有步骤未标注的失败轨迹，即已知轨迹失败但决定性步骤未知。

Method: 提出SOAP（Spectral scOring with Attention-guided Propagation）。首先从LLM表示中构建谱参考空间，依据各步骤的投影行为赋予基础错误分数。针对"下游污染"问题——即后续步骤因继承早期错误而表现出更强失败信号，导致独立打分器偏向下游后果而非决定性错误——SOAP从注意力模式中导出软步骤依赖关系，将下游错误证据向其可能的上游来源传播。

Result: 大量实验表明，SOAP在归因性能上持续优于具有竞争力的基线方法。

Conclusion: SOAP无需步骤级训练标签和单独的成功参考集，通过谱打分与注意力引导的上游错误传播有效缓解下游污染，提升失败归因效果。

Abstract: Failure attribution for LLM agents aims to identify decisive steps responsible for task failure, which is critical for debugging and improving agentic systems. Existing methods rely on costly LLM prompting, step-level error annotations, or verified successful trajectories that lack failure signals for assistance. In this paper, we study failure attribution from step-unlabeled failure trajectories, where each trajectory is known to have failed but its decisive step is unknown. We propose SOAP (Spectral scOring with Attention-guided Propagation), an effective framework requiring neither step-level training labels nor a separate success-only reference set. SOAP first constructs a spectral reference space from LLM representations and assigns each step a base error score according to its projection behavior. Importantly, later steps may exhibit stronger failure signals because they inherit an earlier mistake, causing independent scorers to favor downstream consequences over the decisive error. To address this downstream contamination, SOAP derives soft step dependencies from attention patterns and propagates downstream error evidence toward its likely upstream sources. Extensive experiments show that SOAP consistently improves attribution performance over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [67] [Scaling RNA Foundation Models through Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13106)
*Pengan Chen,Liang Hong,Xinyuan Liu,Han Huang,Ziqian Lin,Jiayang Chen,Yimin Fan,Jiyue Jiang,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Eco-RNA：80M参数RNA基础模型，通过训练后可调迭代次数控制有效深度，以500倍更少参数在RNAGym上超越Evo 2 40B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNA功能由多尺度编码（局部序列motif到长程结构依赖）。现有RNA基础模型将参数容量与推理深度耦合在固定架构中，为每次推理预设固定计算图，缺乏推理计算的灵活性。

Method: 提出Eco-RNA（80M参数），采用共享、输入条件化的Transformer核心，通过重复应用进行迭代精化；迭代次数在训练后选择，在固定参数下控制有效深度。训练方式：跨递归深度训练，结合掩码重建、状态正则化，以及将深层重建锚定到浅层参考的单侧损失。

Result: 在70项assay的RNAGym适应度基准上，精化迭代从1增至4使总体绝对Spearman相关从0.242提升到0.286，增益保持至8次迭代。4次迭代时，Eco-RNA在全部三项聚合适应度指标上超越SOTA Evo 2 40B，且参数少500倍。其迭代配置推进了所评估模型的计算-性能Pareto前沿，并在非编码RNA家族分类、二级结构预测、化学探测谱预测上优于基线。

Conclusion: 内部推理计算可作为RNA基础模型的有效扩展维度。

Abstract: Biological function in RNA is encoded across scales, from local sequence motifs to long-range structural dependencies. Conventional RNA foundation models couple parameter capacity and inference depth within a fixed architecture, prescribing a fixed computation graph for each inference. Here we present Eco-RNA, an 80M-parameter RNA foundation model that makes inference depth adjustable through learned iterative refinement. Its shared, input-conditioned Transformer core refines sequence representations through repeated application. The iteration count is selected after training and controls effective depth at fixed model parameters. We train across recurrent depths with masked reconstruction, state regularization, and a one-sided loss that anchors deeper reconstruction to a shallow reference. On the 70-assay RNAGym fitness benchmark, increasing refinement from one to four iterations raises aggregate absolute Spearman correlation from 0.242 to 0.286, with the gain retained through eight iterations. At four iterations, Eco-RNA surpasses the current SOTA Evo 2 40B on all three aggregate fitness metrics despite 500x fewer parameters. Its iteration configurations advance the compute-performance Pareto frontier among the evaluated models. It also outperforms the evaluated baselines in non-coding RNA family classification, RNA secondary-structure prediction, and chemical probing profile prediction. These results establish internal inference computation as an effective scaling dimension for RNA foundation models.

</details>


### [68] [Continually Post-training Small Models via Synthetic Self-distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13113)
*Woosung Koh,Hyunsoo Lee,Kyungjae Lee,Haeju Park,Dahyun Lee,Dasol Hwang,Moontae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对小型模型持续后训练中"学新忘旧"问题，指出SDFT依赖强上下文学习能力而失效，提出SynthSelf方法，通过外部同族略大对齐模型生成合成自蒸馏监督，实现小模型的有效持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续后训练需兼顾新能力获取与旧知识保留。自蒸馏微调（SDFT）依赖强上下文学习和指令跟随能力，在面向低延迟端侧的小型模型上失效，因此需要在小规模模型上实现持续学习。

Method: 首先定位失败根因：获取差与遗忘都与后训练模型的分布偏移呈线性相关，说明需要分布内学习。据此提出Synthetic Self-Distillation（SynthSelf），将SDFT的分布内重写任务外包给同族、仅略大的对齐模型，并结合离策略监督与拒绝采样，为小模型提供高质量监督而不破坏其原始分布。

Result: 在4个获取数据集（含混合数据）和14个保留基准上评估，SynthSelf在3种小模型规模下持续优于6种方法（含离策略与在策略蒸馏）。

Conclusion: 进一步分析验证SynthSelf为有效的小模型持续学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Continual post-training faces a dual challenge of acquisition and retention: models must learn new capabilities while preserving prior knowledge. While Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning (SDFT) mitigates this using self-supervision with in-context demonstrations, it relies on strong in-context learning and instruction-following abilities. Consequently, SDFT fails on smaller models critical for low-latency, on-device use. To enable continual learning at smaller scales, we first identify the root cause of failure. We show that poor acquisition and forgetting correlate linearly with the post-trained model's distribution shift, revealing the necessity of in-distribution learning. We thus introduce Synthetic Self-Distillation (SynthSelf), imitating SDFT's in-distribution rewrite by outsourcing the task to a minimally larger, same-family aligner. Coupled with off-policy supervision and rejection sampling, this provides the high-quality supervision small models require without breaking their original distribution. Evaluations spanning 4 acquisition datasets (including a mixture) and 14 retention benchmarks show SynthSelf consistently outperforms 6 methods (including off- and on-policy distillation) across 3 small model scales. Extensive further analyses confirm SynthSelf paves the way toward effective small-model continual learning.

</details>


### [69] [WaSA: Wavelet Scattering Autoencoders for Unsupervised Offline Detection of Malicious Clients in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13186)
*Alessandro Licciardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WaSA——一种全无监督、离线的联邦学习恶意客户端检测框架，通过客户端上传的隐私保护谱摘要与仅用干净辅助数据训练的去噪自编码器，在开放IoT场景下识别各类投毒攻击，并引入Boltzmann采样做客户端选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽不暴露原始数据，但仍易受恶意客户端投毒数据攻击。现有防御多为训练期间的被动式或需预先了解攻击类型的监督式，这两类假设在开放式IoT部署中往往不成立，因此需要一种无需攻击标签、可泛化至未知异常的离线性防御方法。

Method: WaSA在训练前让每个客户端向服务器发送一份单一的、隐私保护的谱摘要（spectral summary）；服务器用仅基于干净辅助数据训练的去噪自编码器（denoising autoencoder）学习良性流形，并依据客户端偏离该流形的程度打分，从而识别任意类型的数据污染；不依赖攻击标签，可泛化到未见异常；并提出Boltzmann采样策略用于客户端选择，在硬排除与标准聚合之间平滑插值。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上、多种攻击场景（恶意客户端占比高达90%）中，WaSA相较监督式基线和鲁棒聚合方法取得一致性提升，且通信开销可忽略。

Conclusion: WaSA验证了全无监督、离线检测在开放联邦学习场景中的可行性：借助谱摘要与去噪自编码器的流形偏离评分，可在无攻击标签条件下有效识别恶意客户端，并通过Boltzmann采样兼顾排除与聚合，代码已开源。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without exposing raw data, yet remains vulnerable to malicious clients injecting poisoned datasets. Existing defenses are typically reactive (operating during training) or supervised (requiring prior knowledge of attack types) - assumptions that rarely hold in open-ended IoT deployments. We propose WaSA, a fully unsupervised, offline framework for malicious client detection. Before training, each client transmits a single, privacy-preserving spectral summary of its data to the server. A denoising autoencoder, trained solely on clean auxiliary data, scores clients based on their deviation from the learned benign manifold, identifying corruptions regardless of the attack type.
