Unlike standard residualization or concept erasure, SCCR selectively removes only those directions strongly shared between learned raw and expert embeddings.
  Evaluations on synthetic data, coronary artery disease (CAD) detection, antidepressant treatment dropout prediction, and skin lesion classification using SkinCon annotations show that SCCR identifies the ways in which expert views are insufficient and recovers residual predictive signal while minimizing redundancy with the expert view.

</details>


### [139] [On the Design Space of Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13834)
*Oscar Davis,Daan Roos,Alice Bizeul,Amitis Shidani,Victor Turrisi,Louis Béthune,Pierre Ablin,Marco Cuturi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文聚焦连续扩散语言模型（CDLM）训练中的两个关键杠杆——自条件（self-conditioning）与嵌入几何，揭示其内在机理并据此提出更廉价有效的设计：DoubleCat与SemiCat。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型发展迅速、能力已接近离散扩散，但相比图像/视频等连续模态，学界对训练中真正起作用的因素理解仍然有限，亟需厘清设计空间以指导更优建模。

Method: 1) 分析自条件机制：证明其通过让模型接触训练中罕见的"推理态"来修正训练/推理不匹配；2) 提出两层插值方法DoubleCat，无需额外前向传播即可获得自条件收益；3) 针对大范数高斯主导token的"token可辨识性"问题，引入离散揭示（discrete reveals）得到SemiCat；4) 在嵌入空间上分析交叉熵对嵌入几何的驱动作用；5) 探索混合腐蚀过程与（含异步）噪声调度等设计选项。

Result: DoubleCat将TinyGSM准确率提升三倍以上；SemiCat在TinyGSM上达到49.94%，与离散扩散持平（标准超参加调优采样器）；在1.7B规模上，SemiCat在六项基于似然的评测中四项超越离散扩散，并在无条件生成上优于one-hot flows。嵌入空间中，交叉熵驱使嵌入趋向一种解码错误率与one-hot相近的几何结构。

Conclusion: 自条件本质上是修正训练/推理不匹配，而token可辨识性与嵌入几何是影响CDLM性能的核心因素；据此设计的DoubleCat与SemiCat以更低成本取得更优效果，对理解与推进CDLM设计空间具有参考价值。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models (CDLMs) promise to bring the benefits of continuous flow modelling to language, including accelerated sampling and controllable generation. The field has grown rapidly, and the capabilities of CDLMs have begun to rival those of discrete diffusion. Yet, unlike for continuous modalities such as images or videos, our grasp of the levers that matter when training CDLMs remains limited. In this work, we focus on two of these levers, namely self-conditioning and embedding geometry, and shed light on some of the mechanisms behind them, which in turn suggest cheaper and more effective designs. We begin by showing that self-conditioning corrects a train/inference mismatch by exposing the model to inference-like states that are otherwise rare during training. These gains can be recovered without an extra forward pass by a two-level interpolant, coined $\mathrm{DoubleCat}$, which improves TinyGSM accuracy more than threefold. Self-conditioning also aids in recognising tokens dominated by large-norm Gaussians$\unicode{x2013}$an issue known as token identifiability. To address it directly, we introduce discrete reveals to $\mathrm{DoubleCat}$, yielding $\mathrm{SemiCat}$, which achieves discrete diffusion-level performance on TinyGSM, reaching $49.94\%$ with standard training hyperparameters and a tuned sampler. At the $1.7$B scale, $\mathrm{SemiCat}$ outperforms discrete diffusion on four of six likelihood-based evaluations, and surpasses one-hot flows on unconditional generation. On embedded spaces, we find that cross-entropy drives embeddings towards a geometry whose decoding error rate closely matches the one-hot one. We briefly explore a range of further options, such as hybrid corruption processes and noise schedules, including asynchronous ones. We believe this exploration can help the community better understand and advance the design space of CDLMs.

</details>


### [140] [Specialists Learn Faster: Knowledge Organization in Pre-training Shapes Knowledge Update Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13835)
*Sheng Zhao,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预训练阶段知识的时间组织方式如何影响后续知识更新的效率，发现按时间顺序训练的"专家模型"比随机打乱的"通才模型"更新成本低得多，并从第一性原理推导出更新成本定律，据此设计了预训练数据组织策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多聚焦于训练后如何高效更新模型知识，但忽略了预训练阶段对时间变化知识的组织方式本身可能决定后续更新的效率。作者希望揭示预训练知识组织与更新效率之间的关系，以指导更优的预训练数据排序策略。

Method: 对比两种预训练知识排序：按时间顺序（specialist，专家模型）与随机打乱（generalist，通才模型）。从第一性原理推导更新成本与模型优化动力学之间的关系定律，并用实证结果验证。基于该定律设计兼顾稳定历史知识与高效更新当前知识的预训练数据组织策略，并在真实预训练模型和现有更新工具上验证其泛化性。

Result: 专家模型专精于最近遇到的知识，通才模型在各时间段表现均衡；但专家模型获取新知识所需的更新步数远少于通才模型。推导出的更新成本定律能紧密预测实证结果。所设计的预训练数据组织策略可保留稳定历史知识并高效更新当前知识，且优势可推广到真实预训练模型、现有知识更新工具及其他知识排序方式。

Conclusion: 预训练阶段的时间知识组织方式显著影响后续知识更新效率；通过合理排序预训练数据，可在保持历史知识稳定的同时实现高效更新，对实际模型与工具具有实用价值。

Abstract: An ideal (language) model must continually update its knowledge. Existing work largely focuses on making these updates efficient after training. We investigate how the organization of time-varying knowledge during pre-training shapes the efficiency of subsequent updates. Models trained on chronologically ordered knowledge specialize in the most recently encountered knowledge; we call them specialists. In contrast, models trained on the same knowledge in randomly shuffled order perform well across all represented time periods; we call them generalists. Surprisingly, specialists require far fewer update steps than generalists to acquire new knowledge. To explain this advantage, we derive a law from first principles that relates update cost to the model's optimization dynamics and that closely predicts empirical results. Guided by these findings, we design a strategy for organizing pre-training data that preserves stable historical knowledge while enabling efficient updates to current knowledge. We also show that the benefits of temporal knowledge organization extend to real-world pre-trained models and existing knowledge update tools, and generalize to other knowledge orderings. Project page: https://horizon-lab.org/specialist/

</details>


### [141] [Inference-Time Steering Across Scaffolds and Epitopes for Sample-Efficient De Novo Nanobody Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13844)
*Mason Minot,Simon Friedensohn,Cédric R. Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将生成式分子设计中的条件选择重构为推理时资源分配问题，提出两种动态预算分配器（联合Global SMC与解耦的BaSE），在保持多样性的同时将单位GPU时间的可设计候选产率提升近5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式分子设计通常依赖过生成加事后过滤，效率低。Feynman-Kac（FK）引导等推理时调控方法虽能通过偏向高奖励候选提升效率，但仅在单一固定配置内优化。在从头纳米抗体设计中，预先固定scaffold身份或靶表位等条件选择会形成瓶颈：不同条件下产率差异大，对无前景的配置进行引导会浪费计算资源。因此需要将条件选择视为推理时的资源分配问题。

Method: 提出两种动态预算分配器：1）联合的Global Sequential Monte Carlo（SMC）形式化方法，可跨多个配置同时进行引导与计算资源分配；2）解耦式方法，改编自Batched Successive Elimination（BaSE），以分层淘汰方式分配预算。

Result: 在多个不同治疗靶点与生成配置下，动态分配器将每GPU小时的可设计候选产率提升近5倍，且未牺牲多样性。

Conclusion: 将生成式条件选择重构为推理时资源分配问题，并采用动态预算分配，可显著提升纳米抗体从头设计的计算效率与产率，同时保持候选多样性，为多配置生成设计提供高效方案。

Abstract: Generative molecular design typically relies on over-generation and post-hoc filtering. Inference-time steering methods like Feynman-Kac (FK) steering improve efficiency by biasing trajectories toward high-reward candidates, yet they optimize within a single fixed configuration. In de novo nanobody design, upfront commitment to conditioning choices like scaffold identity or target epitope creates a bottleneck. Since yield varies across these conditions, steering unpromising setups wastes compute. We recast generative condition selection as an inference-time resource-allocation problem. We propose two dynamic budget allocators: a joint Global Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) formulation that steers and allocates compute across configurations concurrently, and a decoupled approach adapting Batched Successive Elimination (BaSE). Empirically, these dynamic allocators increase the yield of designable candidates per GPU-hour nearly 5$\times$ without sacrificing diversity across distinct therapeutic targets and generative configurations.

</details>


### [142] [Partially Informed Stochastic Optimization via Zeroth-Order Residual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13871)
*El Mahdi Chayti,Alireza Mirrokni,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出部分知情随机优化（PISO），将已知梯度分量与残差的在线估计结合，通过零阶观测中心化使探测方差依赖跟踪误差而非残差幅度，从而在非凸光滑目标下取得领先的函数评估复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多优化问题只能直接获得部分梯度，剩余分量须由函数值推断，这一结构出现在表演性预测、表演性强化学习以及下层为黑箱的双层优化中。本文旨在为此类"部分知情"问题设计统一算法并给出收敛保证。

Method: 提出PISO：把可得的梯度分量与残差的运行估计相结合，将每次零阶观测围绕该估计中心化，使随机探测引入的方差取决于跟踪误差而非残差量级；由此得到一个带显式收缩机制的随机草图迭代，并对动量给出原理性解释。每次更新仅需一个探测方向、两个函数样本和一个已知分量样本，并分析了批处理在观测与部署次数之间的权衡。

Result: 对光滑非凸目标，收敛保证仅依赖残差的变差与初始幅度，无需对不可得梯度分量的一致界。在独立采样函数值与高斯或球面探测下，Lipschitz梯度假设下函数评估复杂度为O(d²ε⁻⁶)，附加Lipschitz-Hessian假设下为O(d²ε⁻⁵)。在五类设定中，PISO变体在三种设定下以可比预言机访问超越基线，在双层路由中更快达到可比解，在无响应模型导数的表演性强化学习中表现最佳。

Conclusion: PISO为部分知情梯度场景提供了统一框架，兼具理论复杂度优势与跨多任务的经验效果，并刻画了批处理对部署成本的权衡。

Abstract: Many optimization problems provide direct access to only part of the gradient, while the remaining component must be inferred from function evaluations. This structure arises in performative prediction, performative reinforcement learning, and bilevel optimization with a black-box lower level. We introduce Partially Informed Stochastic Optimization (PISO), which combines the available gradient component with a running estimate of the residual. By centring each zeroth-order observation on this estimate, PISO makes the variance due to random probing depend on the tracking error rather than the residual's magnitude. This yields a randomized sketch iteration with an explicit contraction mechanism and a principled interpretation of momentum. For smooth nonconvex objectives, we establish convergence guarantees that depend on the residual's variation and initial magnitude, without requiring a uniform bound on the unavailable gradient component. With independently sampled function evaluations and Gaussian or spherical probes, PISO attains leading function-evaluation complexities of \(O(d^{2}\varepsilon^{-6})\) under Lipschitz gradients and \(O(d^{2}\varepsilon^{-5})\) under an additional Lipschitz-Hessian assumption. These rates require only one probe direction, two function samples, and one known-component sample per update. The analysis also quantifies how batching trades observations for fewer deployments. Across five settings, PISO variants improve on the tested baselines with comparable oracle access in three, reach a comparable solution faster in bilevel routing, and perform best among methods without response-model derivatives in performative reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [143] [Prediction Is Not a Sufficient Memory State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13881)
*Robert Jenkinson Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了"精确累积学习"与"注意力"中"当前预测"与"未来可学习性"的本质区别：对光滑凸残差损失，存在固定维连续循环状态当且仅当损失为多项式；并给出精确状态维数、压缩误差下界及核注意力的精确闭包分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个能复现当前所有预测的记忆体，可能已经丧失了从下一个观测中继续学习的能力。作者试图精确刻画这一"复现预测"与"累积学习/注意力"之间的区别，即探讨序列学习中所需保留的隐藏状态（证据）究竟有多大，而非提出新的回归算法或声称训练模型上的收益。

Method: 针对无限制实数特征与标签的光滑凸残差损失，分析精确累积学习所需的连续循环状态维数。在普通无权观测下推导degree D损失所需坐标数；对等预测历史进行必要坐标数分析；结合有界标量logistic例子检验固定任意有限导数jet后的障碍；针对岭回归分析隐藏证据（设计矩阵）成本与预测器成本；并在良态零预测族上给出最优B比特压缩的平均单步更新误差下界，最后对核注意力给出精确闭包分类。

Result: 对光滑凸残差损失，固定维连续循环状态存在当且仅当损失为多项式；degree D需精确r=binom(d+D,D)-1个坐标，且在固定当前预测器的历史中仍有r-d个坐标对延续必要；非多项式损失此类等预测历史需求无界。有界标量logistic例子在固定任意有限导数jet后仍保持该障碍。岭回归中精确状态成本为dp+d(d+1)/2（隐藏证据为设计矩阵），而预测器仅需dp。良态零预测族上最优B比特压缩平均单步更新误差量级为d^{-1}2^{-2B/s_d}，其中s_d=d(d+1)/2，即便使用任意随机编码。核注意力给出第二个精确闭包分类。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了未来更新所透露的信息（即学习所需的隐藏证据）而非提出新回归算法或断言模型增益。结果表明：记忆能复现当前预测并不等价于具备持续学习能力，多项式损失是可被有限维连续状态精确闭合的关键边界，岭回归与核注意力均给出精确的状态/闭包分类与压缩下界。

Abstract: A memory that reproduces every current prediction may still have forgotten how to learn from the next observation. We characterize this distinction for exact cumulative learning and attention. For smooth convex residual losses with unrestricted real features and labels, a continuous recurrent state of fixed dimension exists exactly when the loss is polynomial. With ordinary, unweighted observations, degree $D$ requires precisely $r=\binom{d+D}{D}-1$ coordinates; even among histories with one fixed current predictor, exactly $r-d$ coordinates can remain necessary for continuation. Nonpolynomial losses have unbounded requirements on such equal-prediction histories. Bounded scalar logistic examples retain this obstruction after fixing any finite derivative jet. For ridge regression, the hidden evidence is the design matrix: the exact state costs $dp+d(d+1)/2$, versus $dp$ for the predictor. On a well-conditioned zero-prediction family, optimal $B$-bit compression has average one-update error of order $d^{-1}2^{-2B/s_d}$, $s_d=d(d+1)/2$, even with arbitrary randomized codes. Kernel attention gives a second exact closure classification. The results identify what future updates reveal, rather than proposing a new regression algorithm or asserting trained-model gains.

</details>


### [144] [Designing Protein Structures from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13917)
*Joseph Lee,Yanjing Li,Bo Qiang,Gregory Lau,Allyson Ettinger,Noah A. Smith,Rory Donovan-Maiye,David Baker,Hanna Hajishirzi,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prompteina是首个以自然语言为条件、支持全原子蛋白质结构生成的模型，通过构建大规模文本-结构配对数据集并用文本条件化训练，在结构性质、结合体设计及实验验证上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质从头设计模型通常针对每种性质要求专用的输入表示，限制了可支持的设计目标范围。作者希望像文本到图像生成那样，用自然语言统一且灵活地指定结构与功能设计目标。

Method: 构建Prompteina-Data配对数据集（180万结构与超5000万条描述全局及残基特异性结构/功能属性的标注）；基于预训练结构生成模型Proteina-Complexa，通过文本条件化预训练与微调，将残基与其对应文本对齐，实现全原子结构生成。

Result: 在18项结构属性中15项优于基线，生成蛋白更符合目标功能描述；结合体设计中在20个靶点中至少13个领先，界面置信度较最强基线提升14%；迭代反馈下在最难靶点上的增益比次优基线大57%；实验验证GM2A靶点（此前无报道的从头结合体）获得9个结合体，最强表观KD为107 nM。

Conclusion: 结果表明自然语言是一种有效且强大的蛋白质结构生成条件化接口。

Abstract: A central challenge in de novo protein design is generating proteins with desired structural and functional properties. Existing design models typically require a dedicated input representation for each property, limiting the design objectives they support. We develop Prompteina, the first all-atom structure generation model that supports new and broader design objectives specified in natural language, analogous to text-to-image generation. To train Prompteina, we construct Prompteina-Data, a paired text--protein structure dataset of 1.8M structures and 50M+ captions describing global and residue-specific structural and functional properties. We adapt Proteina-Complexa, a pretrained structure generation model, through text-conditioned pretraining and finetuning that aligns residues with their corresponding text. Prompteina outperforms baselines on 15 of 18 structural properties and generates proteins that more closely match descriptions of their intended functions. In binder design, it leads baselines on at least 13 of 20 targets and improves interface confidence by 14\% over the strongest baseline. With iterative in silico feedback, Prompteina's gain over non-iterative generation is 57\% larger than the next-best baseline on the hardest targets. Finally, we experimentally validate Prompteina designs against GM2A, a target with no reported de novo binders. We identify nine binders, with the strongest at an apparent $K_{\mathrm{D}}$ of 107 nM. These results establish natural language as an effective and powerful conditioning interface for protein structure generation.

</details>


### [145] [KL Before and After Mixing: A Round-Complexity Separation in Parallel Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13924)
*Tianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究并行采样器在两种KL散度准则下的采样复杂度分离：调度平均散度 $\mathbb{E}_U D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|q_U)$ 与输出律散度 $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|\mathbb{E}_U q_U)$。对偶数长度 $n$ 的固定权重的二元串均匀目标，前者最小期望轮数为 $\Theta_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$，后者为 $(e^{-\varepsilon}+o(1))\log_2\ln n$，呈现指数级阶差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行采样器可随机选择同轮生成的坐标，但"先随机选调度再采样"与"先采样再按调度平均输出"两种视角会导致不同的散度度量。需辨析 $\mathbb{E}_U D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|q_U)$ 与 $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|\mathbb{E}_U q_U)$ 两种准则下最小轮数复杂度的差异，并刻画最优因果随机策略的精确代价。

Method: 设定刻画：每轮冻结精确条件边际并独立采样，输出前从有限调度混合中抽取调度，且计费每个已调度轮。对偶数长度 $n$、含 $n/2$ 个 1 的二元串均匀分布，给出计数状态递归，精确求解任意轮数上限下、所有因果随机策略的最小输出散度。

Result: 对固定容差 $\varepsilon>0$：调度平均准则下最小期望轮数为 $\Theta_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$；输出律准则下为 $(e^{-\varepsilon}+o(1))\log_2\ln n$，且双对数阶可由每轮次数相同的确定性轮数达到。计数状态递归给出任意轮数上限下的精确最小输出散度。结果可扩展至权重比有界、配额比例远离 0 和 1 的加权固定计数目标（无需置换对称性），以及满支撑 Beta$(1,1)$–Bernoulli 目标，其输出律准则下锐期望代价为 $(e^{-\varepsilon}/2+o(1))\log_2\log_2 n$，由稀有 $n$ 轮运行达到。先前工作已蕴含平衡目标下调度平均代价的阶及两扩展上的 $O_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$ 上界。

Conclusion: 两种散度准则导致并行采样复杂度出现双对数与多项式对数级别的分离，输出律准则显著更优；该分离在加权固定计数与 Beta–Bernoulli 等目标上稳健成立，且计数状态递归可精确刻画最优因果随机策略。

Abstract: A parallel sampler can choose at random which coordinates to generate together. For a target $p$ and the output law $q_U$ of a schedule $U$ drawn afresh for each output, we compare the schedule-averaged divergence $\mathbb{E}_U D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|q_U)$ with the output-law divergence $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p\|\mathbb{E}_U q_U)$, in nats. Rounds commit independent draws from exact conditional marginals frozen at the round's start, each output's schedule is drawn before sampling from a finite mixture of schedules, and every scheduled round is charged. For the uniform distribution on binary strings of even length $n$ with $n/2$ ones, the least expected number of rounds at a fixed tolerance $\varepsilon>0$ is $Θ_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$ under the first criterion but $(e^{-\varepsilon}+o(1))\log_2\ln n$ under the second, and the double-logarithmic order is attainable with the same round count on every run. For this target, a count-state recursion gives the exact least output divergence at every round cap over all causal randomized policies. The separation extends to weighted fixed-count targets with bounded weight ratios and quota fractions bounded away from zero and one, without permutation symmetry, and to the full-support Beta$(1,1)$--Bernoulli target, where the sharp expected cost under the output-law criterion is $(e^{-\varepsilon}/2+o(1))\log_2\log_2n$, attained with rare $n$-round runs. Prior work implies the order of the schedule-averaged cost on the balanced target and its $O_\varepsilon((\log n)^2)$ upper bounds on both extensions.

</details>


### [146] [AI Models Can Provably Hide Arbitrary Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13928)
*Andis Draguns,Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Raymond Douglas,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了将加密神经网络嵌入宿主模型权重的后门攻击，仅在收到秘密触发器时解密并执行隐藏电路，从而在密码学假设下证明这些隐藏能力难以被诱发或解释，揭示了基于诱发的能力审计的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能力评估旨在部署前发现危险的模型行为，但其可靠性依赖于"隐藏能力可被诱发"这一假设。作者试图检验该假设在对抗条件下是否成立。

Method: 利用数字储物柜（digital lockers）技术，将加密的神经网络电路嵌入宿主模型权重中，仅在收到秘密触发器时解密执行；在PyTorch中实现概念验证，并采用微调、权重加噪、基于梯度的触发器搜索及潜在对抗训练等方法进行诱发测试，任务为小规模化学反应预测（作为CBRN相关能力的代理）。

Result: 在密码学假设下，隐藏电路即便在完全访问模型权重的条件下仍可证明难以被诱发和解释；实验表明上述诱发方法均受到抵抗，结合密码学保证，揭示了能力审计的边界。

Conclusion: 基于诱发的能力审计存在根本性局限，作者公开模型以支持防御方法的改进。

Abstract: Capability evaluations aim to surface dangerous model behaviors before deployment, but their reliability depends on the assumption that hidden capabilities can be elicited. We show this assumption does not hold under adversarial conditions by constructing backdoor attacks that embed encrypted neural networks within host model weights, decrypting and executing them only upon receiving a secret trigger. Using digital lockers, these hidden circuits remain provably hard to elicit and interpret under cryptographic assumptions, even given full access to model weights, extending prior work from hiding fixed strings to arbitrary computations. We implement our proof of concept constructions in PyTorch and evaluate elicitation through fine-tuning, weight noising, gradient-based trigger search, and latent adversarial training on a small chemical reaction prediction task - a proxy for CBRN-relevant capabilities. Our results demonstrate resistance to these methods and, together with our cryptographic guarantees, reveal a limit of elicitation-based capability audits. We release our models to support the improvement of defenses.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [CTML: A Comprehensive Framework for Continuous Testing in Machine Learning Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12515)
*Bestoun S. Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CTML框架，面向MLOps环境提供覆盖数据质量、漂移检测、模型质量与流水线验证四维度的自动化持续测试方案，并通过预测性维护案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统在生产中的广泛部署暴露出传统软件测试方法的不足：ML系统因依赖数据而存在特有失效模式，如数据漂移、概念漂移和性能衰减，传统测试手段难以覆盖这些问题。

Method: 提出Continuous Testing for Machine Learning（CTML）框架，专为MLOps环境设计，提供标准化、自动化的评估方案。框架覆盖四大测试维度：数据质量、漂移检测、模型质量与流水线验证；采用模块化架构，包含Test Registry、Test Runner、Result Store和Integration Layer，可与现有CI/CD流水线及MLOps平台无缝集成。通过在预测性维护案例中的应用进行实证验证。

Result: 在预测性维护案例研究中，CTML有效识别了数据漂移、性能退化及流水线瓶颈等传统测试方法容易忽略的问题，在全部测试维度上成功发现系统关键故障，为保障ML系统生产稳定性提供了有效洞察。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一个新的集成化框架，将学术研究与工业最佳实践相连接，推动了MLOps质量保障标准的演进。

Abstract: The increasing use of machine learning (ML) systems in production has highlighted some important weaknesses in traditional software testing methods. Unlike regular software, ML systems can fail in specific ways due to their reliance on data, including issues like data drift, concept drift, and performance decline. To address these issues, this paper introduces the Continuous Testing for Machine Learning (CTML) framework. This framework is designed specifically for MLOps environments and offers a thorough, automated solution. It establishes a standardized way to evaluate ML systems across four main areas: data quality, drift detection, model quality, and pipeline validation. The CTML framework consists of a modular setup that includes a Test Registry, Test Runner, Result Store, and Integration Layer. This design ensures it integrates smoothly with existing Continuous Integration/Continuous Delivery (CI/CD) pipelines and MLOps platforms. We put this framework to the test in a predictive maintenance case study, which showed how effective it is at identifying issues like data drift, performance degradation, and bottlenecks in the pipeline problems that traditional testing methods might overlook. The findings indicate that CTML successfully uncovers critical failures in the system across all testing dimensions, providing useful insights to help keep ML systems running smoothly in production. Overall, this work presents a new, integrated framework that connects academic research with industry best practices, pushing forward the standards in MLOps quality assurance.

</details>


### [148] [Fix What's Wrong, Keep What's Right: Hallucination Repair for LLM-Based Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12529)
*Jiageng Wu,Chao Shen,Zihan Guo,Junyan Shao,Zongyan Zhang,Jie Song,Zhong Li,Mingyang Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出首个免训练的事后幻觉修复框架 OPT-Kraken，用于修复 LLM 优化建模产物中的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 被用于自动化运筹优化建模，但生成的建模产物易与原始问题不一致，产生"优化建模幻觉"。通用幻觉缓解方法难以应对此类问题，因为需要模型-代码协同更新以恢复对原问题的保真度。

Method: OPT-Kraken 为无需训练的事后修复框架：提取问题需求与实例数据；通过检查问题-模型保真度、模型-代码一致性、数据接地来定位错误；根据受影响范围选择修复粒度；对局部修复，将类别规则与实例证据编译为"契约"，规定允许的编辑、预期效果与受保护内容。

Result: 在既有优化建模基准上，方法对多种自动建模框架和微调模型均取得正向增益；在多个基座模型的零样本产物上，能有效修复幻觉并保留原本正确的产物。

Conclusion: 结果表明 OPT-Kraken 可对基于 LLM 的优化建模产物实现有效且可控的幻觉修复。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored to automate operations research modeling, reduce modeling time and lower the expertise barrier to optimization based decision support. However, this process often introduces inconsistencies between generated artifacts and the original problem, which we refer to as optimization modeling hallucinations. Despite progress in general-purpose hallucination mitigation, optimization modeling poses a distinct repair challenge: restoring fidelity to the original problem through coordinated model-code updates. We introduce OPT-Kraken, a training-free hallucination repair framework. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first post-hoc framework for repairing hallucinations in LLM-based optimization modeling artifacts. It extracts problem requirements and instance data, localizes errors by checking problem-model fidelity, model-code consistency, and data grounding, and selects repair granularity by the affected scope. For local repair, category rules and instance evidence are compiled into contracts specifying permitted edits, expected effects, and protected content. Experiments on established optimization modeling benchmarks show positive gains across automatic modeling frameworks and fine-tuned models. On zero-shot artifacts from multiple base models, OPT-Kraken effectively repairs optimization modeling hallucinations while preserving initially correct artifacts. These results demonstrate effective and controlled repair for LLM-based optimization modeling artifacts.

</details>


### [149] [Quantifying SMT Solver Instability for Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12649)
*Daniel Evans,Rylie Hansen,John Shorter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文使用变异系数在三个软件领域的大规模SMT查询数据集上量化SMT不稳定性，发现其普遍显著且随平均运行时间增加而加剧；并提出一种新量化方法表明：若以运行时间而非方差为优化目标来优化查询结构，可在缓解不稳定性的同时提升求解器整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMT求解器对程序验证的自动化至关重要，但SMT不稳定性（语义等价的代码排列导致求解时间大幅延长或超时）会中断工作流，使软件工程师倾向回避验证。已有工作因运行时间分布异常而难以量化该现象，因此需要更合适的度量方法并探索缓解途径。

Method: 采用变异系数（coefficient of variation）来建模不稳定性，覆盖来自三个软件领域的大规模SMT查询数据集；并提出一种新的不稳定性量化方法，用于比较以运行时间和以方差为优化目标的不同策略。

Result: 结果显示SMT不稳定性在三个领域中均十分显著，且随查询平均运行时间的增加而增大；借助新量化方法发现，若针对运行时间而非方差优化查询结构，不稳定性可被缓解，同时求解器整体性能也能得到提升。

Conclusion: 不稳定性是SMT求解中普遍且随耗时增加而恶化的问题；通过以运行时间为目标的查询结构优化，有望在降低不稳定性的同时改善整体求解性能，为后续验证工具的设计提供新方向。

Abstract: Program verification has become commonplace in software development projects, and much of its feasibility can be attributed to the potential of SMT solvers to automate model checking and other verification techniques. However, SMT instability, a phenomenon where semantically equivalent permutations of source code take much longer to solve or timeout, has been found to interrupt workflows and make software engineers more likely to avoid verification. Previous efforts to measure SMT instability have had difficulty quantifying it due to the abnormal runtime distributions it produces. In this paper, we use the coefficient of variation to model instability across an extensive dataset of SMT queries from three software domains. Our results show that SMT instability is significant in each area, and that it increases for queries that take longer on average. However, using a novel method to quantify instability, we show that a different approach is possible: if a solution were to optimize the structure of queries for runtime, rather than variance, instability could be mitigated while also improving overall solver performance.

</details>


### [150] [Leakage-Resistant Evaluation of AI-Generated Database Specifications in Specification-Driven Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12778)
*Oleg Grynets,Ivan Fedorchuk,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出抗泄漏框架与基准LRFB，用于评测AI生成Oracle PL/SQL规格说明的评估器，包含494个已标注的源-规格对、19项质量指标（7个维度），并通过金丝雀测试与标签置换检验保证基准有效性。三个LLM评判者与两个平凡基线对比，在247条留出记录上的平衡准确率未显著优于规格长度基线，但能提供长度基线所不具备的缺陷类型与证据定位信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规格说明评估研究多聚焦于二分类问题检测，缺乏对评估器本身抗泄漏性与有效性的系统控制。当基准的标注信息可能通过推理泄漏时，评估结果的可信度难以保证；同时，单一的二分类指标无法刻画评估器能否给出有据可查、分门别类的缺陷发现。因此需要一个带基准有效性控制的评测框架，并重新审视评估目标的设定。

Method: 构建LRFB框架与基准：494个带标签的源-规格对，覆盖19项质量指标、归入7个维度；每项指标26条记录，分为各13条的开发集与留出集，每子集含3条干净规格与10条缺陷规格。真值经机制化密封以隔离推理，并用金丝雀测试与标签置换测试校验。评测3个LLM评判者，每条记录运行3次，与2个平凡基线对比，采用精确配对检验判断差异显著性，并额外统计缺陷类别的发现命中率与引用证据与标注跨度的重合度。

Result: 在247条留出记录上，GPT-4o、DeepSeek V4 Pro、Claude Sonnet 4.5的平衡准确率分别为0.87、0.89、0.81，而规格长度基线为0.88；精确配对检验显示没有评判者与该基线存在显著差异。但评判者提供了长度基线无法提供的信息：被违反记录中有84%–92%的发现命中标注的缺陷类别，引用证据与标注跨度的重合度为92%–93%。

Conclusion: 平衡准确率上的"无显著差异"表明二分类问题检测指标可能被长度等平凡信号主导，不足以区分评判者能力。研究据此主张将规格说明评估的目标从二分类问题检测转向证据关联的、带类型的缺陷发现，并强调基准有效性控制（防泄漏、金丝雀与标签置换检验）是评测可信度的必要前提。

Abstract: This paper presents a leakage-resistant framework and benchmark (LRFB) for measuring evaluators of AI-generated Oracle PL/SQL specifications. The benchmark contains 494 labelled source-specification pairs across 19 quality metrics grouped into seven dimensions; each metric contains 26 records divided into development and held-out subsets of 13 records each, with three clean and ten defective specifications per subset. Ground truth is mechanically sealed from inference and checked with canary and label-permutation tests. Three LLM judges were evaluated with three runs per record and compared against two trivial baselines. On 247 held-out records, GPT-4o, DeepSeek V4 Pro, and Claude Sonnet 4.5 reached balanced accuracies of 0.87, 0.89, and 0.81, respectively, while a specification-length baseline reached 0.88. Exact paired tests did not separate any judge from that baseline. The judges nevertheless provided information unavailable to length: the labelled defect category appeared among findings in 84%-92% of violated records, and quoted evidence overlapped the labelled span in 92%-93%. The study therefore reframes specification evaluation from binary issue detection toward evidence-linked, typed findings and benchmark-validity controls.

</details>


### [151] [Co-Modifying Application Code and Deployment Infrastructure: A Causal Study of AI Agent Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12849)
*Mohammad Wathiq Soualhi,Basem Suleiman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过26,999个PR的匹配分析揭示：智能体在双域PR（同时修改应用代码与部署基础设施）中呈现双模态集成结果——显式审查者面临+13.13小时中位延迟、合并时间增至3.24倍；而43.1%–56.2%的双域PR绕过人工审查，中位31分钟内静默合并，危及构建验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件工程智能体主要在隔离沙箱中针对同步本地测试进行评估，但其在协作仓库中部署功能时，需同步修改CI/CD流水线、容器与配置清单。由于智能体缺乏部署感知（deployment awareness），跨域修改会把未经验证的依赖引入异步集成流水线中，因此需要量化双域PR在集成环节的因果开销。

Method: 评估两个大型数据集（AIDev v5与MOSAIC-3M）中的26,999个PR，覆盖六种智能体架构及对齐的人类基线。采用确定性四层文件分类法与精确4D分层匹配框架，在条件可交换性假设下、控制应用变更量的前提下分离集成指标。

Result: 智能体主要充当应用编码者或杂务工作者，维护类任务占其PR的17.70%–25.41%；双域修改仅占智能体PR的4.48%–4.55%，却触发双模态结果：显式审查下带来+13.13小时中位惩罚、合并时长×3.24，机制上由CI/CD与配置清单驱动；另一模态中，即便外部贡献者无写权限，智能体身份仍使人工审查几率较人类同行降低63.7%，致使43.1%–56.2%的双域PR绕过检查、中位31分钟内静默合并。

Conclusion: 未被审查的双域修改损害了构建验证，表明需要部署感知型基准（deployment-aware benchmarks）以及严格的基于路径的访问控制。

Abstract: Current software engineering agents are evaluated predominantly inside isolated sandboxes against synchronous local tests. However, deploying features in collaborative repositories requires modifying continuous integration (CI/CD) pipelines, containers, and provisioning manifests alongside application logic. Because agents lack deployment awareness, attempting cross-domain modifications introduces unverified dependencies into asynchronous integration pipelines. To isolate the causal integration overhead of dual-scope pull requests (PRs)-modifying both application code and deployment infrastructure-we evaluated 26,999 PRs across two large datasets (AIDev v5 and MOSAIC-3M) encompassing six agent architectures and an aligned human baseline. Using a deterministic four-tier file taxonomy and an exact 4D strata-matching framework, we isolated integration metrics under conditional exchangeability while controlling for application volume. We find agents operate primarily as application coders or chore workers, with maintenance tasks capturing 17.70% to 25.41% of agent PRs. Dual-scope modifications represent only 4.48% to 4.55% of agentic PRs, yet trigger bimodal integration outcomes. In the first mode, explicitly reviewed contributions experience protracted delays, adding a median penalty of +13.13 hours and multiplying time-to-merge by 3.24x, driven mechanically by CI/CD and provisioning manifests. In the second mode, maintainer gatekeeping exhibits a structural bypass: even for external contributors lacking repository write access, agent identity reduces the odds of human review by 63.7% compared to human peers. Consequently, 43.1% to 56.2% of dual-scope PRs bypass inspection to merge silently in a median of under 31 minutes. These uninspected modifications compromise build verification, demonstrating the need for deployment-aware benchmarks and strict path-based access controls.

</details>


### [152] [Evaluation of Software Quality Through Refactoring Commit-Impact Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12890)
*Md Safayat Hossen,Sheikh Moonwara Anjum Monisha,Shadid Intisar Quasem*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过挖掘GitHub开源仓库并结合多种静态代码分析工具，对重构提交前后代码修改与软件质量指标的关联进行实证分析，并以表格和图形汇总结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在实证检验代码修改（尤其是重构提交前后的变更）与软件质量之间的关联，弥补该方面实证证据的不足。

Method: 以GitHub开源软件为对象，采用仓库挖掘与静态代码分析工具；围绕规模、复杂度、代码异味、漏洞和缺陷检测等软件度量指标，比较每次重构提交前后的变更；通过设定研究问题并借助相应工具获取数据，再以表格和图形对结果进行汇总与分析。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化结果，仅说明所得结果已汇总并以表格和图形进一步分析，用于支撑案例研究结论。

Conclusion: 摘要未明确陈述结论，仅表明通过上述实证流程可回答所提出的研究问题并完成案例研究。

Abstract: The aim of this study is to conduct an empirical analysis of the correlation between code modifications and software quality. This will be achieved by examining the effects of changes made before and after each refactoring commit, using software metrics such as size, complexity, code smells, vulnerability, and bug detection. The objective is to conduct static code analysis on open-source software from GitHub repositories using various repository mining and static code analysis tools. Our approach involves formulating research-based questions and utilizing the specified tools to obtain results that will enable us to resolve the case study. The obtained results have been summarised and further analyzed using tables and graphs to achieve the objective.

</details>


### [153] [SCCGBench: Executability-Oriented Evaluation for Real-World Web Service-Call Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12920)
*Shuo Wang,Guodong Fan,Ming Zhu,Jing Li,Chun Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SCCGBench，一个包含 3,135 个可追溯、去污染过滤的 RapidAPI 调用函数基准，用于在真实代码环境下评估 LLM 生成服务调用代码的调用与执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有服务计算中 LLM 生成服务调用代码的方法尚未在真实代码环境中评估服务的调用与执行，缺乏执行落地的评测手段，且存在循环泄漏问题。

Method: 构建 SCCGBench 基准，采用 redacted-intent generation 降低循环泄漏；引入 Mock、Robust、ParamF1 及 host/endpoint/method/parameter 级诊断指标；在 intent-only、retrieval-augmented、gold-documentation Oracle 三种设置下评估 6 个代码 LLM 与 1 个非神经检索基线。

Result: 模型常生成语法正确或表面相似的 HTTP 请求代码，但 intent-only 下 Mock 成功率低，最强模型仅 3.76%；Oracle 设置下开源模型 Mock 达 88.73%–90.52%，DeepSeek-V4-Pro 为 76.47%–81.86%；稀疏与稠密检索远低于 Oracle 水平。

Conclusion: 服务/API grounding 是主要瓶颈，Mock 主要由 host、endpoint、method 对齐决定，ParamF1 单独揭示参数对齐错误；SCCGBench 为 Web 服务调用、API 推荐、服务接口检索、endpoint grounding 与可靠服务调用自动化提供执行落地的基准。

Abstract: LLM-generated service-call code is increasingly important for service computing, where developers rely on large language models (LLMs) to compose services and invoke third-party services. However, current approaches have not yet evaluated the invocation and execution of services in the actual code environment. To support execution-grounded evaluation, we present the Service-Call Code Generation Benchmark (SCCGBench), a benchmark of 3,135 traceable and decontamination-filtered RapidAPI call functions from real GitHub repositories. SCCGBench uses redacted-intent generation to reduce circular leakage and introduces Mock, Robust, ParamF1, and host-, endpoint-, method-, and parameter-level diagnostics. Mock denotes a virtual-execution hit that simultaneously matches the gold host, endpoint, and HTTP method, while Robust further requires status checking and exception handling. We evaluate six code LLMs and one non-neural retrieval baseline under intent-only, retrieval-augmented, and gold-documentation Oracle settings. Results show that models often generate syntactically correct or superficially similar HTTP-request code but achieve low Mock success in the intent-only setting; the strongest evaluated model obtains only 3.76% Mock. Under the gold-documentation Oracle settings (C3 and C5), Mock reaches 88.73%--90.52% for the open-source models evaluated in RQ2 and 76.47%--81.86% for DeepSeek-V4-Pro, whereas sparse and dense retrieval remain far below this oracle level. The findings indicate that service/API grounding is the main bottleneck: Mock is dominated by host, endpoint, and method alignment, while ParamF1 separately reveals parameter-alignment errors. SCCGBench provides an execution-grounded benchmark for Web service invocation, API recommendation, service-interface retrieval, endpoint grounding, and reliable service-call automation.

</details>


### [154] [Don't Guess, Just Ask: Evaluating Interactive Requirement Clarification for Repository-Level Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13022)
*Mingyu Chen,Zhiqing Zhong,Manyi Wang,Linxi Liang,Rouyu Chen,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ReqClarifyBench，一个面向仓库级软件开发（游戏、小程序、Web）的交互式需求澄清基准，包含 341 个任务与基于真实对话人设的 LLM 用户模拟器，评估了六个 LLM 的澄清有效性与需求文档质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 实现软件代码的能力日益增强，但真正的瓶颈在于其是否理解用户需求。用户通常以模糊意图起步，关键需求需通过交互澄清；而在仓库级开发中，误解会传播并导致高昂返工。现有基准多基于完整规格或欠规格请求直接评测实现，忽视了编码前的需求澄清；且既往澄清研究主要聚焦函数级任务。因此，LLM 能否通过交互将模糊意图转化为清晰需求、以及该能力如何影响后续代码生成，尚不明确。

Method: 作者构建了 ReqClarifyBench，覆盖游戏、小程序与 Web 开发共 341 个任务，每个任务包含模糊用户意图、经人工验证的需求文档以及代码评估工件。评测采用基于真实世界对话衍生出多样人设（personas）的 LLM 用户模拟器进行交互；对六个 LLM 从澄清有效性与生成需求文档质量两个维度进行评估。

Result: 实验显示，更强的澄清有效性并不必然带来更高的需求文档质量；不同用户人设下模型表现差异显著；此外，与各自的基础 LLM 相比，编码智能体框架（coding-agent harnesses）并未进一步提升需求文档质量。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了预编码需求澄清是仓库级 LLM 软件开发中被忽视的关键环节。澄清能力与文档质量之间并非简单正相关，人设敏感性与智能体框架收益有限等发现，为构建 LLM 驱动的端到端软件开发系统提供了重要参考。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly capable of implementing software, a critical bottleneck emerges: whether they truly understand what users want. Users often begin with vague intent, leaving important requirements to be clarified through interaction. In repository-level development, misunderstandings can propagate and cause costly rework. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate implementation from complete specifications or underspecified requests, overlooking pre-coding requirement clarification. Prior clarification studies mainly focus on function-level tasks. It therefore remains unclear how well LLMs can transform vague intent into clear requirements through interaction and how this capability affects subsequent code generation.
  We introduce ReqClarifyBench, an interactive requirement clarification benchmark for repository-level software development. It contains 341 tasks spanning game, miniapp, and web development, each with a vague user intent, a human-validated requirement document, and code evaluation artifacts. We employ LLM-based user simulators with diverse personas derived from real-world conversations. We evaluate six LLMs in terms of clarification effectiveness and generated requirement document quality. Results show that stronger clarification effectiveness does not necessarily translate into higher document quality, and performance varies substantially across user personas. We also find that coding-agent harnesses do not further improve requirement document quality compared with their base LLMs. These findings offer insights for building LLM-based end-to-end software development systems.

</details>


### [155] [TestSuiteBench: Coding Agents Struggle to Generate Strong Test Suites from Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13065)
*Kazuki Egashira,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Mark Niklas Müller,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TestSuiteBench，一个包含 165 个开源应用的测试套件生成基准，旨在评估编码智能体从零为完整软件生成测试套件的能 力。结果显示前沿智能体生成的测试套件变异分数低于 60%，暴露出"能构建软件却无法验证软件"的能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成基准已扩展到完整应用级别，但测试生成基准仍局限于单个函数或缺陷修复，从零为复杂软件生成完整测试套件的任务尚未被探索。这一空白使得无法评估智能体在自主软件开发中的验证能力。

Method: 构建 TestSuiteBench 基准，包含 165 个开源应用，每个应用配有：结构化的预期行为规格说明、参考实现、参考测试套件，以及平均 74 个变异体。每个变异体由规格中的单一需求生成并精炼以隔离该行为：73% 的变异体仅被一个参考测试杀死，平均仅被剩余测试的 0.2% 杀死。基于该基准评估前沿编码智能体的测试套件生成能力。

Result: 前沿编码智能体即使给定规格说明，也难以生成强测试套件，变异分数低于 60%；尽管其套件大体有效，88% 的测试能在参考实现上通过。生成的测试套件往往缺少相关输入组合，规模也小于参考套件。同时，针对相同规格生成的实现能通过 82% 的参考测试。

Conclusion: 智能体能够构建软件却无法验证软件，这一能力缺口直接限制了完全自主的软件开发。测试套件生成是当前编码智能体的关键薄弱环节，需在自主软件工程流程中加以重视。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly used to create large software projects. While code-generation benchmarks have scaled accordingly to full applications, test-generation benchmarks remain limited to individual functions or bug fixes. In particular, generating complete test suites for complex software from scratch remains unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce TestSuiteBench, a benchmark of 165 open-source applications, each with a structured and detailed specification of intended behavior, a reference implementation, a reference test suite, and on average 74 mutants. Each mutant is generated from a single requirement of the specification and refined to isolate that behavior: 73\% are killed by exactly one reference test and, on average, by only 0.2\% of the remaining tests. We find that even frontier coding agents struggle to generate strong test suites given our specifications, achieving mutation scores below 60\%, despite their suites being largely valid and 88\% of their tests passing on the reference implementation. We observe that generated test suites often lack relevant input combinations and are smaller than reference test suites. Meanwhile, implementations generated for the same specifications pass 82\% of the reference tests. These results show agents can build software they cannot verify, a gap that directly limits fully autonomous software development.

</details>


### [156] [Agent4Spec: An LLM-based Agentic System to Optimize API Specifications through Automated Validation and Market Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13273)
*Yongjian Tang,Costel Moraru,Doruk Tuncel,Hanya Elhashemy,Steffen Klepke,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Agent4Spec：面向API规范持续验证与市场驱动演进的多智能体框架，含验证与市场分析两条流水线，实现语法错误自纠正与功能缺口自动补齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API规范是软件开发的关键文档，直接影响集成效率与客户端代码生成。现有基于LLM的方法将规范生成视为一次性事件，忽视了持续验证、长期维护与市场驱动演进的需求。

Method: 提出Agent4Spec自主多智能体框架，包含两条流水线：（1）验证流水线，检测并自我纠正OpenAPI语法错误；（2）市场分析流水线，开展竞争性调研、识别相对行业领先者的功能缺口，并自动生成缺失的API规范功能。

Result: 在两个涵盖通用与工业场景的精心构建的API规范数据集上评估。在9个SOTA LLM中，表现最优者实现100%语法错误检测率、超过95%的修正实现成功率，以及超过50%的端点改进建议采纳率。

Conclusion: Agent4Spec将API规范从一次性生成转变为持续验证与演进的闭环过程，是面向API开发的实用工具。

Abstract: API specifications serve as critical documentation for modern software development, directly impacting integration efficiency and client code generation. While recent advancements have leveraged Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate initial specifications from source code, existing approaches treat this process as a one-time event - overlooking the needs for continuous validation, long-term maintenance, and market-driven evolution of these artifacts. To address this issue, we present Agent4Spec, an autonomous multi-agent framework featuring two distinct pipelines: (1) a validation pipeline that detects and self-corrects OpenAPI syntax errors, and (2) a market analysis pipeline that conducts competitive research, identifies functional gaps against industry leaders, and automatically generates missing API-spec functionalities. We evaluate Agent4Spec on two curated datasets of API specifications spanning both general-purpose and industrial scenarios. Among 9 SOTA LLMs, the top-performer achieves 100% syntax error detection rates and over 95% implementation success for proposed corrections, while securing over 50% adoption rates for suggested endpoint improvements, representing a practical tool for API development.

</details>


### [157] [AgentSWE: Can Coding Agents Build the Agent You Actually Want?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13361)
*Jiahao Wang,Hongjin Qian,Yuyang Hu,Jiajun Zhang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 AgentSWE 基准，覆盖编码智能体开发全生命周期的三个阶段（创建、编辑、优化），共 25 个任务，以"委托"而非"考试"的形式评估交付的智能体是否真正满足需求，而非仅仅看似满足。实验发现当前各类编码智能体均难以交付需求所要求的结果，"演示与交付之间的差距"是常态而非个例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件生产逐渐交由软件自身完成，编码智能体被越来越多地委托去构建适配用户自身工作流的智能体。这种委托隐含一个假设：交付的智能体将忠实服务于其被构建的需求，而非仅表面上满足。然而现有基准均未检验这一假设。

Method: 提出 AgentSWE 基准，涵盖智能体开发生命周期的三个阶段：依据自然语言需求创建完整智能体、编辑生产级智能体代码库、朝可度量目标进行优化。25 个任务均以"委托"形式构建：构建者可针对少量示例用例迭代，验收时则在覆盖完整需求的留出用例上运行冻结的提交，依据需求推导的评分标准在硬性通过/失败门限下评分（优化类任务则依据源基准自身的客观指标）。评估覆盖多种模型与执行框架（harness）的编码智能体。

Result: 没有任何编码智能体能接近交付需求所要求的结果；"构建者所演示的"与"其实际交付的"之间的差距是普遍现象而非边缘情况。对失败轨迹的实证分析识别出若干反复出现的失败模式：对可见用例的过拟合、代码看似完整但运行时空洞、以及无支撑的完成声明。

Conclusion: AgentSWE 首次检验了"编码智能体交付的智能体是否忠实满足需求"这一被忽视的假设，揭示了当前智能体软件工程能力的显著缺口，并指出上述失败模式可作为编码智能体学习构建其被要求构建的智能体的信号。

Abstract: As the production of software passes into the hands of software itself, coding agents are increasingly commissioned to build agents fitted to their users' own workflows. This delegation carries a quiet assumption, that the delivered agent will faithfully serve the requirement it was built for, rather than merely appear to. No existing benchmark tests that assumption. We introduce AgentSWE, a benchmark for agent software engineering spanning three stages of the agent development lifecycle: Creation of a complete agent from a natural-language requirement, Editing of production agent codebases, and Optimization toward a measurable target. Each of its 25 tasks is framed as a commission rather than an exam: the builder iterates against a handful of illustrative cases, while acceptance exercises the frozen submission on held-out cases designed to span the full requirement, grading behavior against requirement-derived rubrics under hard pass/fail gates or, for Optimization, against the source benchmark's own objective. Evaluating a range of coding agents across models and harnesses, we find that none comes close to delivering what a requirement asks for: the gap between what a builder demonstrates and what it delivers is the norm, not the corner case. An accompanying empirical analysis of the failure trajectories identifies recurring failure modes, among them overfitting to the visible cases, hollow runtimes behind code that looks complete, and unsupported claims of completion, each a signal by which coding agents can learn to build the agents they are asked for.

</details>


### [158] [ProgramGym: Scaling Training Environments for Long-Horizon Program Reproduction Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13395)
*Chengyi Yang,Renxu Bao,Jue Hou,Zongpei Teng,Ziheng Wang,Yujie Lin,Chengsong Huang,Junnan Zhu,Delai Qiu,Yining Wang,Shengping Liu,Fei Long,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ProgramGym 提出一套面向长程程序复现智能体的可扩展训练框架，包含构建可验证环境的 Program-Env 与角色隔离工作流的 Program-Agent，在 ProgramBench 上显著提升程序复现性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体多用于修复仓库问题或按需求文档生成仓库，而"从零构建程序"这一更贴近真实软件工程的场景缺少可扩展、可验证的训练环境；同时现有智能体工作流难以支撑"先探索参考程序再实现"的长程开发过程。

Method: 提出 ProgramGym 框架，由两部分组成：(1) Program-Env：通过反馈驱动的循环，修复运行时沙箱构建与黑盒测试质量，从代码仓库自动构建可验证训练环境，共获得覆盖 C、C++、Go、Rust 的 1,648 个训练环境；(2) Program-Agent：将分析、实现、审查角色隔离的工作流与版本修订管理相结合，支持编译检查与失败恢复。

Result: 在 ProgramBench 上，Program-Env 与 Program-Agent 均提升程序复现性能。使用所构建环境对 Qwen3.8-27B 进行轨迹微调，性能从 34.87% 提升至 42.84%；Program-Agent 进一步将基座模型与 SFT 模型分别提升至 55.71% 和 56.25%，均超过部分旗舰模型。

Conclusion: ProgramGym 为"从零构建程序"的编码智能体研究提供了可扩展的可验证训练环境与有效工作流，作者将开源该框架以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly used to build programs from scratch as software engineers do rather than to resolve issues within a repository or to generate a repository from a given requirements document. However, scalable verifiable training environments that force agents to discover what is required and to decide how the system should be designed remain scarce. Moreover, existing agent workflows also cannot sustain long-horizon development in which the agent explores the reference program before implementation. To address these challenges, we introduce ProgramGym, a framework consisting of Program-Env and Program-Agent for scalable training environments of long-horizon program reproduction agents. Specifically, Program-Env constructs verifiable training environments from repositories through a feedback-driven loop that repairs runtime sandbox builds and the quality of black-box tests. Using this novel pipeline, we obtain 1,648 training environments spanning C, C++, Go, and Rust. Program-Agent combines a role-isolated workflow of analysis, implementation, and review with revision management for compilation checks and failure recovery. Experiments on ProgramBench show that both Program-Env and Program-Agent improve performance on program reproduction. Fine-tuning on trajectories with these constructed environments raises the performance of Qwen3.8-27B from 34.87% to 42.84%. Program-Agent further raises the base and SFT models to 55.71% and 56.25%, both exceeding some flagship models. We will release the ProgramGym to support further research on coding agents that build programs from scratch.

</details>


### [159] [REINS: Agent-guided Thread Scheduling for Concurrency Bug Detection and Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13421)
*Yu Zhang,Corina S. Pasareanu,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 REINS，一种基于智能体（agentic）的并发缺陷检测与复现方法。它利用智能体对并发语义和已知并发缺陷模式的推理来指导线程调度，通过运行时观测与静态信息结合形成缺陷假设，并转化为事件顺序与变量状态约束以引导受控调度器探索，从而在有限预算内更高效地触发缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发缺陷仅在特定线程交错下才会显现，难以检测和复现。受控并发测试技术虽接管线程调度以探索交错空间，但该空间随程序规模迅速增长，在有限预算内找到能触发缺陷的交错仍具挑战。程序并发语义可揭示在特定交错下哪些变量访问与同步操作会导致异常行为，从而帮助聚焦于与缺陷相关的交错，但既有技术尚未充分利用此类语义信息。

Method: REINS 的核心是用智能体对并发语义与已知并发缺陷模式的推理来指导线程调度。方法从运行时观测出发，结合静态信息推理程序并发行为并形成潜在缺陷假设；每个假设转化为对事件顺序和变量状态的一组约束，用于引导受控调度器趋向可能触发缺陷的交错；随后根据执行结果得出结论，不断精化约束，直到缺陷被触发或假设被证伪。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，与现有技术相比，REINS 在相同时间预算内检测到更多缺陷。此外，REINS 在真实项目中发现了六个此前未知的并发缺陷，其中三个已获开发者确认。

Conclusion: REINS 表明将并发语义推理与受控调度相结合，能够更有效、更有针对性地探索缺陷相关交错，在有限预算下提升并发缺陷的检测与复现能力，并具备在真实项目中发现未知缺陷的实用价值。

Abstract: Concurrency bugs manifest only under specific thread interleavings, making them hard to detect and reproduce. Existing controlled concurrency testing techniques take over thread scheduling to explore the interleaving space. As the space grows rapidly with program scale, finding interleavings that can trigger bugs within a limited budget remains challenging. The program's concurrency semantics can reveal which variable accesses and synchronization operations under specific interleavings lead to unexpected program behavior, and thus help focus the exploration on bug-relevant interleavings. However, prior techniques have not fully exploited such semantic information. In this paper, we present REINS, an agentic approach for detecting and reproducing concurrency bugs. At its core, REINS guides thread scheduling with the agent's reasoning about the concurrency semantics and known concurrency bug patterns. REINS starts with runtime observations and combines them with static information to reason about the program's concurrency behavior and formulate hypotheses about potential bugs. A hypothesis translates into a set of constraints on event orders and variable states, guiding a controlled scheduler toward interleavings likely to trigger the bug. REINS draws conclusions from the execution results and keeps refining the constraints until the bug is triggered or the hypothesis is refuted. Compared with existing techniques on three public benchmarks, REINS detects more bugs within the same time budget. Furthermore, REINS found six previously unknown concurrency bugs in real-world projects, three of which have been confirmed by the developers.

</details>


### [160] [Not All Correct Fixes Run Alike: An Empirical Study of Patch Performance in Repository-Level Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13563)
*Han Peng,Xing Hu,Feifei Niu,Huan Li,Xin Xia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究能否复用 SWE-bench 等仓库级 issue 解决基准中内置的 FAIL_TO_PASS（F2P）测试来评估补丁的运行时性能。作者用 4 个前沿模型配合 SWE-agent 在 SWE-bench 与 SWE-bench-Live 的可测量语料上产出 262 个含至少两种正确解的案例，以指令数与墙钟时间度量性能，并用 Welch ANOVA 替代 RSD 作为判别准则。结果显示该方法在近一半实例中能区分补丁性能差异，且性能表现与模型解决率无关、模型排名近乎相反，性能分析还能揭示正确性评估遗漏的架构退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级基准（如 SWE-bench）主要以补丁能否通过正确性测试来评判，不同通过测试的补丁之间运行时性能差异基本未被考察。为每个实例专门设计性能负载成本高昂，而基准自带的 F2P 测试恰好覆盖了 issue 修复所影响的行为，因此作者希望探究能否将其改造为性能评估负载。

Method: 选取 Claude Opus 5、GPT 5.6、DeepSeek V4 Pro、Qwen 3.8 Max 四个前沿模型，搭配 SWE-agent，在 SWE-bench 与 SWE-bench-Live 已验证切分中预筛选的可测量语料上求解，得到 262 个至少包含两种不同正确解（含开发者 ground-truth 补丁）的案例。对每个解在原生 F2P 测试上测量指令数与墙钟时间，并采用 Welch ANOVA 作为比此前工作所提 RSD 更稳健的统计判别准则。

Result: 该度量方法在运行时性能上具有显著判别能力，在近一半实例中识别出补丁间的性能差异。实证研究表明，生成补丁的运行时性能与 LLM 的 issue 解决率相互独立，模型排名近乎相反。案例研究还发现，性能分析能揭示某些补丁中被正确性评估忽略的架构退化。

Conclusion: 解决率并不足以刻画 LLM 的仓库级 issue 解决能力，未来评估应将补丁性能与解决率一并纳入考量。

Abstract: Repository-level issue-resolution benchmarks such as SWE-bench evaluate submitted patches primarily based on whether they pass the correctness tests, leaving the runtime performance of different test-passing patches largely unexplored. Evaluating patch performance is essential but challenging since designing performance workloads for each benchmark instance is costly. However, these benchmarks already provide a natural candidate: the built-in \emph{FAIL\_TO\_PASS} (F2P) tests, which exercise the behavior affected by the issue fix. We therefore investigate whether the provided F2P tests can be repurposed to evaluate patch performance. Overall, we choose four frontier models, Claude Opus~5, GPT~5.6, DeepSeek V4 Pro, and Qwen~3.8 Max, paired with SWE-agent to resolve the pre-filtered measurable corpus from verified splits of SWE-bench and SWE-bench-Live, yielding 262 cases with at least two different correct solutions, including the developer ground-truth patch. We then measure each solution's runtime performance in instruction count and wall-clock time on the native F2P test, and use Welch ANOVA instead of the RSD proposed in prior work as a more robust statistical discrimination criterion. We find that this methodology exhibits notable discrimination capability in runtime performance and identifies performance differences among patches in almost half of the instances. Our empirical study shows that the runtime performance of generated patches is independent of the issue resolution rate of LLMs, with nearly reversed model rankings. The case study also shows that the performance analysis reveals architectural regression in some patches that the correctness evaluation overlooked. These findings suggest that resolution rate does not adequately characterize LLMs' capability for repository-level issue resolution, motivating future evaluations to consider patch performance alongside it.

</details>


### [161] [SWE-DeceptionBench: Benchmarking Deceptive Behaviors of LLMs Across the Software Development Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13626)
*Xiting Liu,Lin Yang,Yitong Zhang,Jia Li,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-DeceptionBench，用于评测大语言模型在软件工程中的欺骗行为，覆盖六个生命周期阶段、两类欺骗触发方式及三种外部条件，共720个中英测试实例，发现主流模型欺骗率较高且现有轻量防御效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在软件开发生命周期中被广泛使用，其可信度问题日益突出，欺骗行为（给出误导性回答，或协助编造、隐瞒、扭曲信息）是其中尤为重要的风险，但目前缺乏针对软件工程场景的系统性评测基准。

Method: 构建SWE-DeceptionBench基准：覆盖软件生命周期六个阶段；定义两类欺骗触发——主动欺骗（模型识别出需求不可行或相互冲突，却仍宣称可实现）与被动欺骗（模型协助完成用户明确的误导他人请求）；并设置Neutral、Reward、Pressure三种条件以考察外部诱因影响；共生成720个中英文测试实例，并对七种主流LLM进行评测，同时评估轻量级防御方法。

Result: 即使在Neutral条件下欺骗仍普遍存在，主动欺骗总体发生率为49.88%，被动欺骗为64.29%；欺骗率随生命周期阶段而变化；外部诱因对主动与被动欺骗的影响程度不同；轻量防御中，guard模型漏检大量欺骗性请求，Self-Reminder虽能降低但无法消除欺骗性回答。

Conclusion: LLM在软件工程中的欺骗行为广泛存在且难以通过现有轻量防御彻底缓解，亟需更有效的欺骗检测与缓解方法，以支撑可信的LLM驱动软件工程。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used throughout the software development lifecycle. This growing reliance on LLMs raises concerns about their trustworthiness, with deception posing a particularly important risk. Deceptive behavior takes the form of misleading responses or assistance in fabricating, concealing, or distorting information. To study this behavior, we introduce SWE-DeceptionBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM deception in software engineering. The benchmark covers six lifecycle stages and two deception triggers: Active Deception, where models recognize infeasible or conflicting requirements but still present them as achievable, and Passive Deception, where models help fulfill explicit user requests to mislead others. We further introduce Neutral, Reward, and Pressure conditions to study the effect of external inducements, resulting in 720 test instances in Chinese and English. Evaluating seven mainstream LLMs, we find substantial deception even under the Neutral condition, with aggregate rates of 49.88% for Active Deception and 64.29% for Passive Deception. Deception rates vary across lifecycle stages, and external inducements affect Active and Passive Deception to different extents. We also evaluate lightweight defenses, finding that guard models miss many deceptive requests, and Self-Reminder reduces but does not eliminate deceptive responses. These findings highlight the need for more effective deception detection and mitigation methods to support trustworthy LLM-based software engineering.

</details>


### [162] [A-SAW: The Agentic Software Architecture Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13647)
*Finn Klatt,Luca Di Grazia,Johannes Schneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 A-SAW（Agentic Software Architecture Workflow），在 Architecture as Code 基础上引入常驻智能体层：IDE 集成智能体依据版本控制的 C4 模型、类型化元素目录与插桩模板起草架构文档，每份草稿必须经过人工评审门禁。在大型工业组织中以两条路径评估：45 份架构文档的评分量表评估（含 13 对匹配的源-再生文档对）与 13 次任务锚定的从业者访谈。结果显示该工作流消除了导致质量不可预测的作者间方差，内在质量中位数提升 +19 分；智能体生成文档在完整性（100%）、一致性（93%）与机器可读性（99%）上达到或接近量表上限，可追溯性为 79%。13 名受访者中 11 名拒绝未经人工评审的智能体输出，1 名警告精良输出可能诱导评审者跳过审查。研究支持保留强制人工评审，并将其集中于智能体必须超越代码仓库进行推断之处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中的 AI 辅助集中于实现与验证，设计阶段（尤其是架构文档维护）相对未被充分服务。Architecture as Code 虽使架构制品可版本化、可 diff、可机器读取，但并未降低其更新成本，设计文档仍是碎片化、依赖作者、信任度有限的记录。因此需要一种在流程层面解决架构文档持续维护与质量一致性的方法。

Method: 提出 A-SAW，一种流程级制品：在 Architecture as Code 之上扩展常驻智能体层。IDE 集成智能体基于三方面输入起草架构文档——版本控制的 C4 模型、类型化元素目录、插桩模板；每份草稿在采纳前必须通过强制人工评审门禁。在大型工业组织中开展双轨评估：（1）对 45 份架构文档进行评分量表评估，其中包含 13 对匹配的源-再生文档对；（2）13 次任务锚定的从业者访谈。此外说明该队列平均 58% 与 89% 的捆绑模板、目录、智能体及更丰富再生上下文，用以自上界界定智能体贡献而非隔离其作用。

Result: A-SAW 消除了导致质量不可预测的作者间方差，内在质量在匹配文档对上中位数提升 +19 分。智能体生成文档中：完整性 100%、一致性 93%、机器可读性 99%，均达到或接近评分量表上限；可追溯性达 79%。访谈中 13 名参与者有 11 名拒绝未经人工评审的智能体输出；1 名警告精良输出可能使评审者跳过审查。

Conclusion: 研究支持保留强制人工评审，并将其集中用于智能体必须超越代码仓库进行推断的环节。A-SAW 在流程层面有效提升架构文档质量与一致性，但结果对智能体贡献构成上界而非隔离估计，人工评审仍是必要保障。

Abstract: AI assistance in software engineering focuses on implementation and verification, leaving the design phase, and in particular the maintenance of architecture documentation, comparatively underserved. Architecture as Code makes architectural artifacts versionable, diffable, and machine-readable, but it does not reduce the effort of keeping them updated, so design documentation still ends up as a fragmented, author-dependent, and only partially trusted record of why a system looks the way it does.
  In this paper we introduce the Agentic Software Architecture Workflow (A-SAW), a process-level artifact that extends Architecture as Code with a persistent agent layer: an IDE-integrated agent drafts architecture documents from a version-controlled C4 model, a typed element catalog, and instrumented templates. Every draft passes a mandatory human review gate before it is accepted. We evaluate it in a large industrial organization along two tracks: a rubric assessment of 45 architecture documents including 13 matched source-regeneration pairs, and 13 task-anchored practitioner interviews. Our results show that the workflow collapses the between-author variance that made quality unpredictable. Intrinsic quality also rises, by a median of +19 points across the matched pair. The cohort has a mean of 58% and 89% bundle templates, catalog, agent, and a richer regeneration context. So they bound the agent's contribution from above rather than isolating it. In the agent-generated documents, completeness (100%), consistency (93%), and machine-readability (99%) reach or approach the rubric maximum, while traceability reaches 79%. Eleven of the 13 participants rejected agent output that no human had reviewed, and one warned that polished output may lead reviewers to skip the review. Our work supports keeping human review mandatory and concentrating it where the agent must infer beyond the repository.

</details>


### [163] [(When) Should a Software Factory be Lights-off? Benchmarking Code Review Agents on Bug Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13668)
*Chenyang Yang,Hao He,Ti-Chung Cheng,Jenna Butler,Emerson Murphy-Hill,Carmen Badea,Christian Bird*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 CRBugBench 基准测试，用仓库历史中的人工验证缺陷及其引入 PR 构建 1000 个开源评审任务与 800 个企业专有任务，系统评估自动代码评审智能体的缺陷检出能力，并给出采用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着编码智能体普及，开发者花在评审上的时间超过编写代码，人工评审速度已跟不上智能体生成代码的节奏，因此业界倾向于构建自动化评审智能体、追求"关灯式"无人监督软件工厂。但这类全自主模式的可行性尚缺乏可靠评估手段，尤其缺少以人工确认缺陷为金标准、可衡量真实评审检出率的基准。

Method: 构建 CRBugBench：(1) 设计数据流水线，从仓库历史中挖掘缺陷，并将每个缺陷与其引入该缺陷的 PR 配对，形成以 PR 为输入的评审任务；(2) 基准包含 98 个开源 GitHub 仓库的 1,000 个评审任务，以及来自某大型公司专有行业仓库的 800 个附加任务；(3) 在基准上评测 SOTA 评审智能体（如 claude-opus-5 + copilot-cli、opus-4.6 等），以 top-5 评论内的缺陷检出率为主指标，并分析复杂变更、非局部推理需求、项目上下文缺失等因素的影响，同时考察测试时算力扩展（尤其提示词策略）对检出率与成本的权衡。

Result: SOTA 评审智能体表现良好：最佳组合（claude-opus-5 + copilot-cli）在 top-5 评论内检出 71.9% 的缺陷，五个月模型演进中由 opus-4.6 的 47.7% 显著提升。但智能体在复杂变更和需要非局部推理的缺陷上效果较弱，在更依赖项目上下文的专有行业仓库上性能明显下降。算力扩展最有效的途径是提示词改变：探索引导型提示在成本增加 10% 的情况下带来最高 5.8% 的性能提升。

Conclusion: 全自主"关灯式"软件工厂目前尚不现实，评审智能体可作为有效辅助但需人工监督与针对性适配。据此提出具体落地指南：按 PR 复杂度调节评审投入、用项目优先级引导智能体、提供仓库知识、依据 PR 特征调度不同模型，并随模型演进持续监控评审质量。

Abstract: With the advances of coding agents, software developers are spending more time reviewing code than writing code. As human review velocity falls behind agentic code generation, it is tempting to build automated review agents towards a future of "lights-off" software factory with little to no human supervision. To assess the practicality of such a fully autonomous software factory, we construct CRBugBench, a new benchmark that evaluates whether review agents detect human-verified bugs. We build a data pipeline that mines bugs from repository history, and pair each bug with its introducing PR to form a review task. CRBugBench consists of 1,000 review tasks across 98 open-source GitHub repositories, and 800 additional tasks from proprietary industry repositories at one large company.
  We then use the benchmark to study agent review quality: We found that SOTA review agents do well: the best (claude-opus-5 with copilot-cli) catches 71.9% of bugs within its top-5 comments, up from 47.7% (opus-4.6) in five months of model evolution. Yet agents remain less effective on complex changes and bugs requiring non-local reasoning, with sharp performance drops on proprietary industry repositories where project context is more often needed. To improve bug detection, we found scaling compute helps most through prompt changes: exploration-nudging prompts gain up to 5.8% performance at 10% higher cost. From these findings, we derive concrete guidance for developers to adopt review agents - scaling review effort to PR complexity, steering agents by project priorities, supplying repository knowledge, dispatching models by PR characteristics, and monitoring quality as models evolve.

</details>


### [164] [MARGoS: A Configuration-Driven Platform for Reproducible MARL Experiments in ARGoS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13772)
*Tom Philipp Backert,Mohammad D. Soorati,Javad Ghofrani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出MARGoS——一个轻量级、配置驱动的平台，将ARGoS+MARL实验的定义性输入（配置、种子、路径、环境信息等）提升为平台管理的一等工件，通过配置哈希、端到端种子传播、配置冻结、结构化日志、环境指纹与可导出实验包提升多智能体强化学习群体机器人实验的可复用性与可复现性，并以架构、效率、可复现性、可用性、协作五个维度进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARGoS模拟器被广泛用于研究群体涌现行为，但MARL群体机器人领域的复用与可复现支持系统性不足且缺乏标准化：单个实验需手动对齐模拟器文件、训练脚本、配置值、路径、种子与环境细节，即便在同一研究组内部，复用与复现也很脆弱。

Method: 提出MARGoS，一个轻量级、配置驱动的平台，把实验定义性输入作为平台管理的一等工件，提供配置哈希、端到端种子传播、冻结（不可变）配置、结构化日志、环境指纹，以及可导出实验包，用于跨机器与跨时间的共享、重放与结果验证。

Result: 通过架构、效率、可复现性、可用性、协作五个维度的评估，支持其主张：将实验变体管理视为平台职责，可使ARGoS+MARL实验更易于复用、复现与交接。代码已开源于https://github.com/tombackert/margos。

Conclusion: MARGoS证明由平台统一管理实验变体是提升ARGoS+MARL实验可复用性、可复现性与可交接性的有效途径，为MARL群体机器人研究提供了可操作的标准化工具支撑。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) has become a prominent research direction, particularly within collective robotics, where the ARGoS simulator is widely used to study emergent swarm behavior. Yet despite this growth, systematic support for reuse and reproducibility in MARL-based swarm robotics remains limited and insufficiently standardized: a single experiment is assembled from manually aligned simulator files, training scripts, configuration values, paths, seeds, and environment details, so reuse and reproduction stay fragile even within one research group. To address this gap, we introduce MARGoS, a lightweight, configuration-driven platform developed in this project that elevates these experiment-defining inputs to first-class, platform-managed artifacts. MARGoS enables reproducible experimentation through configuration hashing, end-to-end seed propagation, frozen (immutable) configurations, structured logging, environment fingerprinting, and exportable experiment bundles that facilitate sharing, replay, and verification of results across machines and over time. A five-dimension evaluation (architecture, efficiency, reproducibility, usability, and collaboration) supports our claim that managing experiment variation as a platform responsibility makes ARGoS+MARL experiments easier to reuse, reproduce, and hand off. Our code is available at https://github.com/tombackert/margos.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Beyond the Final Embedding: Retrieving Memories from Token-Level Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13277)
*Han Luo,Zhan Zhuang,Xu He,Jujia Zhao,Yu Li,Suqin Yuan,Dong Li,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种稀疏token匹配的免训练检索增强方法，将查询token与候选记忆token的局部匹配分数与整体相似度结合排序，可选低秩查询适配器在固定编码器下进行有监督微调，在八个基准上提升召回与QA准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于嵌入的检索器多用单一向量表示比较查询与记忆，但当相关性仅依赖内容的一小部分（局部证据）时，单向量表示难以捕捉，导致检索效果受限。

Method: 利用同一嵌入模型已计算的上下文token表示，采用稀疏token匹配：每个查询token在候选记忆中检索少量匹配token，将匹配分数按记忆聚合，并与整体记忆相似度加权组合用于排序；可选的低秩查询适配器可在编码器与已存储记忆表示固定的条件下进行有监督训练。

Result: 在八个基准和多个模型家族上无需额外训练即可提升检索性能；加入查询适配后在Qwen3-Embedding-4B上平均Recall@10与NDCG@10分别提升13.3和14.3个百分点；检索增益进一步转化为长期记忆基准上的问答准确率提升。

Conclusion: 稀疏token匹配是一种免训练、通用有效的检索增强策略，结合可训练低秩查询适配器可进一步放大收益，并带动下游问答性能提升。

Abstract: Memory-augmented assistants retrieve evidence from stored conversations and documents to answer queries. Most embedding-based retrievers compare single-vector representations of queries and memories, even when relevance depends on a small part of the content. Such local evidence can be matched to queries using the contextual token representations already computed by the same embedding models. Our method uses sparse token matching to improve retrieval without additional training. Each query token retrieves a small set of matching tokens across candidate memories. The matching scores are aggregated for each memory and combined with whole-memory similarity to rank memories. An optional low-rank query adapter can be trained with supervision while keeping the encoder and stored memory representations fixed. Our method improves retrieval across eight benchmarks and multiple model families without additional training. Query adaptation further increases the gains to 13.3 and 14.3 percentage points in average Recall@10 and NDCG@10 on Qwen3-Embedding-4B. These retrieval gains translate into higher question-answering accuracy on long-term memory benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [PRIME: Pre-Retrieval Index Modeling Enhancement for Structured Document Retrieval-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13296)
*Cheuk Hei Chung,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出索引侧优化框架PRIME，通过语义重构与元数据合成/层次检索两条路线提升检索质量并大幅降低索引成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息检索研究主要优化查询侧检索器与生成器，将语料侧表示简化为分块，导致索引侧质量这一优化杠杆被忽视。

Method: PRIME框架包含两条互补路线：一是LLM驱动的语义重构（SR），将长文档转化为结构化、信息密集的段落；二是确定性的元数据合成（MS）与层次检索（HR），抽取语义锚点与标题结构以实现由粗到细的段落检索。

Result: 确定性元数据在匹配结构感知分块的检索质量同时，将嵌入计算降低45.8倍、索引向量数降低22.4倍；语义重构在全量部署中进一步将Answer-F1提升13.7%。在QASPER、BRIGHT、NFCorpus上刻画了决定索引侧优化成败的三类定性机制，并表明强重排器下这些索引侧效应被中和。

Conclusion: 索引侧优化是被低估的有效杠杆，可在保质量的同时显著降低索引成本，但其收益依赖于特定机制且在强重排器下会被抵消。

Abstract: High-precision retrieval over external knowledge bases is essential for LLM agents to operate effectively. However, existing Information Retrieval (IR) research primarily optimizes query-side retrievers (e.g., ColBERT, BGE) and generators (e.g., Self-RAG, GraphRAG), while reducing corpus-side representation to chunking. This leaves index-side quality an underexplored lever. We propose Pre-Retrieval Index Modeling & Enhancement (PRIME), an index-side optimization framework built on two complementary tracks: an LLM-driven Semantic Restructuring (SR) track that transforms long documents into structured, information-dense sections, and a deterministic Metadata Synthesis (MS) and Hierarchical Retrieval (HR) track that extracts semantic anchors and heading structure for coarse-to-fine section retrieval. Our evaluations show that deterministic metadata matches structure-aware chunking's retrieval quality while cutting embedding computation by 45.8 $\times$ and index vector count by 22.4 $\times$. On top of these gains, Semantic Restructuring (SR) further improves Answer-F1 by 13.7% in full deployment, with characterized quality-fidelity-cost trade-offs. Across QASPER, BRIGHT, and NFCorpus, we further characterize three qualitative regimes that determine whether index-side optimization helps or degrades retrieval, while showing that these index-side effects are neutralized under a strong reranker.

</details>


### [167] [NEAR: Neighborhood-Aware Residuals for Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13314)
*Han Luo,Zhan Zhuang,Xu He,Jujia Zhao,Yu Li,Suqin Yuan,Dong Li,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架NEAR，利用邻居共享信息构建残差表示以增强查询-记忆匹配，提升长上下文检索与问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存储记录间常因共享主题、实体或事件而产生内容重叠，导致缺乏所需证据的记录获得高相似度分数，从而压过正确证据。

Method: NEAR为每条记忆从其邻居推导参考方向，查询从预计算表中获取参考；基于这些参考为查询和记忆构建投影残差，并将残差与原始嵌入结合，在固定记忆索引上执行单次近似最近邻搜索。

Result: 在八个长上下文检索基准和九种嵌入模型上取得一致性提升，下游问答性能亦得到改善。

Conclusion: 利用记录间共享信息构造的残差表示可有效补充原始嵌入，在免训练条件下提升检索与下游问答表现。

Abstract: Memory retrieval aims to identify evidence from stored records to answer a query. Stored records with different factual details may share topics, entities, or events. Such shared contents would make records without the required evidence more likely to receive high similarity scores and outrank the correct evidence. In this paper, we use the information shared by these records to construct local references for query-memory matching. We propose NEighborhood-Aware Residuals (NEAR), a training-free framework that uses these references to construct residual representations that complement the original embeddings. Specifically, each memory derives a reference direction from its neighbors, and each query obtains its reference from a precomputed table. Using these references, NEAR constructs projection residuals for both queries and memories. These residuals are combined with the original embeddings for a single approximate nearest-neighbor search over a fixed memory index. Experiments on eight long-context retrieval benchmarks show consistent improvements across nine embedding models. Downstream evaluations show that NEAR improves question-answering performance.

</details>


### [168] [SPARQL-LLM in the TEXT2SPARQL 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13648)
*Panayiotis Smeros,Vincent Emonet,Ruijie Wang,Ana-Claudia Sima,Tarcisio Mendes de Farias*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SPARQL-LLM是一个开源KGQA系统，在TEXT2SPARQL 2026挑战赛中综合排名第一，准确率最多高出第二名59%，支持英德西多语言问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过SPARQL查询分布式知识图谱难度大，需要掌握查询语法、端点规范与模式细节。KGQA系统可自动将自然语言转为SPARQL，而LLM的进展使该翻译日益可行。

Method: 提出开源KGQA系统SPARQL-LLM，利用大语言模型将自然语言问题自动翻译为SPARQL查询，并在TEXT2SPARQL 2026挑战赛中进行评测，支持多语言问答。

Result: 在八个参赛方法中综合排名第一；相比第二名准确率最高提升59%；支持英语、德语和西班牙语；系统已开源并部署于去中心化生物信息学知识图谱的生产环境。

Conclusion: SPARQL-LLM验证了LLM驱动的KGQA在多语言、多数据集场景下的有效性，具备开源与生产部署的实用价值。

Abstract: Querying distributed data represented as Knowledge Graphs (KGs) via SPARQL remains a difficult task, requiring fluency in query syntax, endpoint specifications, and schema details. Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA) systems address this by automatically translating natural language into SPARQL, and recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have made this translation increasingly feasible. In this paper, we describe SPARQL-LLM, our open-source KGQA system, in the context of the TEXT2SPARQL 2026 challenge. In this challenge, SPARQL-LLM ranks first overall among eight competing approaches across all datasets, achieving up to 59% higher accuracy than the second-best approach and supporting multilingual question answering in English, German, and Spanish. SPARQL-LLM is publicly released at https://github.com/sib-swiss/sparql-llm and deployed in production over decentralized bioinformatics KGs at https://www.expasy.org/chat.

</details>


### [169] [Tracing and Taming Hallucinated Terms via Subsequence-Association Filtering and Breadth-Aware Selection for LLM Query Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13810)
*Amit Kumar Jaiswal,Sagar Uprety,Prayag Tiwari*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出免训练、检索器无关的查询扩展方法SAFE-QE，基于子序列关联假说识别并降权幻觉扩展词，在TREC/BEIR上以更低幻觉词注入量达到与主流方法相当的nDCG@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为查询扩展词来源会产生幻觉，注入不存在的实体和离题词，引入排序噪声并损害强检索器。现有方法（语料接地、互验证、偏好对齐）将幻觉词视为无差别噪声，缺少对某一词为何出现的机制性解释，也缺乏保留多少词的原理性准则。

Method: SAFE-QE为免训练、检索器无关方法。1) 依据"子序列关联"幻觉解释，以语料条件下的跨上下文可复现性对每个候选扩展词打分：在随机化查询上下文下反复出现但在伪相关集中缺乏支持的词，被判为占主导的幻觉关联产物并降权；2) 将语言生成刻画为一致性（无幻觉）与广度（覆盖度）的权衡，把词选择建模为对伪相关集的约束覆盖最大化问题，保留在重采样下近似广、一致且稳定的词集；3) 提出"幻觉扩展词率"(HETR)查询侧忠实度指标，统计没有相关文档支持的注入扩展词。

Result: 在TREC和BEIR基准上，SAFE-QE在nDCG@10上与生成式及语料引导扩展统计不可区分，但注入的不受支持词更少：每查询4.3个，而Query2doc为27.8、HyDE为57.5；在强学习稀疏检索器上，所有扩展方法均出现回退。忠实度在排序质量上无显著代价。

Conclusion: 幻觉词可通过跨上下文可复现性与伪相关集支持度加以区分并降权，词选择可形式化为约束覆盖最大化问题；由此可在不损失排序质量的前提下获得更高的扩展忠实度，且方法免训练、无需修改检索器。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now a standard source of query expansion terms but they hallucinate. They inject non existent entities and off-topic terms that add ranking noise and can hurt strong retrievers. Existing remedies such as corpus grounding, mutual verification and preference alignment treat hallucinated terms as an undifferentiated nuisance to be filtered by agreement heuristics, without a mechanistic account of why a given term appears or a principled criterion for how many terms to keep. We propose Subsequence-Association Filtered, breadth-aware query expansion (SAFE-QE), a training-free, retriever agnostic method that draws on the subsequence-association account of hallucination. We score each candidate expansion term by its corpus conditioned cross-context reproducibility, terms that reappear under randomised query contexts yet lack support in the pseudo-relevant set are flagged as products of dominant hallucinatory associations and down-weighted. Drawing on the characterisation of language generation as a trade-off between consistency (no hallucination) and breadth (coverage), we cast term selection as constrained coverage maximisation over the pseudo-relevant set, keeping a set that is approximately broad but consistent and stable across resampling. We also introduce the Hallucinated Expansion Term Rate, a query side faithfulness metric that counts injected expansion terms no relevant document supports. We evaluate our approach on benchmarked TREC and BEIR collections. Our SAFE-QE is statistically indistinguishable from generative and corpus-steered expansion on nDCG@10 while injecting fewer unsupported terms 4.3 per query against 27.8 for Query2doc and 57.5 for HyDE and on a strong learned sparse retriever, where every expansion method regresses. Faithfulness is therefore available at no measurable cost in ranking quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Faithfulness Without Anchors: A Position Paper on the Structural Misalignments of Explainable AI Faithfulness Metrics for Feature Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12493)
*Marco Zullich,Emily Schiller,Ivan Gentile,David Dembinsky,Adriano Lucieri,Kilian Göller,Steffen Seitz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出可解释AI(XAI)在提升信任方面整体失效，主要因解释与模型间存在不忠实(unfaithfulness)；现有忠实性度量存在五大缺陷，作者提出四个研究方向以提升其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI常被宣传为通过使算法更易理解来提升对机器学习模型的信任，但十年来的证据表明该目标总体失败，反而助长对错误预测的过度依赖，并可能导致算法厌恶。XAI实际主要作为调试工具。其核心问题在于XAI方法常过度简化模型内部机制，导致解释与模型之间出现错位，即不忠实/不保真(unfaithfulness/infidelity)。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文(position paper)，采用论辩与批判性综述的方式，而非提出新算法。作者将忠实性界定为解释与模型之间的近似程度，并系统梳理当前忠实性度量指标的局限，归纳出五个主要缺陷，并据此提出四个可能的研究方向。

Result: 文章识别出当前忠实性度量指标的五大主要缺陷，并指出这些缺陷导致对忠实性的量化要么不可行、要么计算代价高昂。据此提出四个提升其可靠性的研究路径。

Conclusion: 严格计算忠实性可量化解释可信度中的一个要素；然而现有度量不足，需沿作者提出的四个方向改进，以支撑更可靠的忠实性评估。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) tools have often been presented as methods to increase trust in machine learning models by making aspects of their algorithms more human-understandable. However, evidence accumulated over the last decade points to an overall failure of this objective, instead promoting overreliance on incorrect predictions, which can subsequently lead to algorithmic aversion. Rather, XAI appears to function primarily as a debugging tool, useful for obtaining anecdotal insights into, for example, features learned by the model. One of the main issues underlying this limitation is that XAI methods often oversimplify the inner workings of the model, resulting in misalignment between the explanation and the model itself - a behavior referred to as unfaithfulness or infidelity. Faithfulness can be interpreted as the degree of approximation between an explanation and the model. Computing faithfulness in a rigorous manner would thus make it possible to quantify one of the various elements of trustworthiness of an explanation. However, existing metrics present significant limitations, making this quantification either infeasible or computationally expensive. In this position paper, we outline five major shortcomings of current faithfulness metrics and propose four possible avenues for research to bolster their reliability.

</details>


### [171] [EliteTable: Self-Improving Program Search for Executable Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12536)
*Stuart Farmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EliteTable 是一种由大语言模型引导的自改进程序搜索算法，通过固定容量的高分方案表来指导新方案生成、编辑与重组。在六个 Gymnasium 控制任务上，搜索过程中大多改进，部分任务上优胜方案在新回合中有迁移增益，但相对优势与组件贡献待验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用大语言模型进行程序搜索时，需要一个机制有效积累与复用高分方案，以持续引导搜索并提升所得方案的迁移表现。

Method: EliteTable 维护固定容量的高分方案表，并以该表引导三类操作：新方案提议、已有方案编辑、多个方案的重组。评估覆盖六个 Gymnasium 控制任务，每任务十代、每代五十个候选槽，每任务进行十次独立初始化搜索，并观察候选得分中位数、优胜者搜索得分中位数与配对留出端点变化。

Result: 六个任务的候选得分中位数从第一代到末代均上升；五个任务的优胜者搜索得分中位数上升。CartPole-v1 中，十次搜索的优胜者在第一代即达到搜索得分上限。六个任务中有四个的十个配对留出端点全部改善。BipedalWalker-v3 与 CarRacing-v3 有改善但未达报告目标。Acrobot-v1 十次搜索中有六次在留出回合上下降，配对变化中位数为负。

Conclusion: 结果记录了程序搜索过程中的改进，以及在部分任务中将优胜者增益迁移到新回合的现象。仍需对比实验与消融实验来确定 EliteTable 的相对性能及各组件贡献。

Abstract: EliteTable is a self-improving program-search algorithm guided by large language models (LLMs) that uses a fixed-capacity table of high-scoring solutions to guide new proposals, edits of existing solutions, and remixes of several solutions. Performance was evaluated across six Gymnasium control tasks, each with ten generations and fifty candidate slots per generation. Each task was evaluated across ten independently initialized searches. Candidate-score medians rose between the first and final generations in all six tasks, and selected-winner search-score medians rose in five. In CartPole-v1, the selected winner reached the search-score ceiling in the first generation of all ten searches. All ten paired held-out endpoints improved in four of the six tasks. BipedalWalker-v3 and CarRacing-v3 improved without reaching their reporting targets. Six of ten Acrobot-v1 searches declined on held-out episodes, and the median paired change was negative. The results document improvement during program search and the transfer of selected-winner gains to new episodes in some tasks. Comparisons and ablations are needed to establish EliteTable's relative performance and the contributions of its components.

</details>


### [172] [Locate, Judge, Read: Role-Specialized Tool Agents over a Knowledge-Point Index for Open-Domain Multimodal Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13128)
*Wenlin Lin,Fanzhe Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowForge 将检索增强问答拆分为检索（Locator）与阅读（Deep Reader）两个专职智能体，配以代码调度的桥接判断与类型化证据池，在 M3DocVQA 等三个基准上显著超越既有 RAG/GraphRAG 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强智能体让同一模型同时持有检索与阅读工具并自行调度，导致其在多跳、异构文档集合上倾向于反复检索而非真正阅读证据。作者认为"找段落"与"读段落"是两类不同工作，应由不同智能体分工完成。
